  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmakathā</i> [<a href="#entry-kathaa" class="skt-link">kathā</a>] <a href="#entry-raamakathaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. histoire de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; elle est contée not. dans le <a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rāmāyaṇa</a>, le <a href="#entry-Uraamacaritamaanasa" class="skt-link">Rāmacaritamānasa</a>, le <a href="#entry-Uraamacarita" class="skt-link">Rāmacarita</a>, et le <a href="#entry-Uraamopaakhyaana" class="skt-link">Rāmopākhyāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamalifgezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmaliṅgeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>-<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-raamalifgezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Rāmaliṅgeśvara, l'un des 12 signes radieux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> [<a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a>], établi sur l'ile de <a href="#entry-Uraamezvara" class="skt-link">Rāmeśvara</a>; <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> le consacra [<a href="#entry-lifgaprati_s_thaa" class="skt-link">liṅgapratiṣṭhā</a>] à son retour de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> avec <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a>, en route vers <a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a>; il y expia le crime de <a href="#entry-brahmahatyaa" class="skt-link">brahmahatyā</a>, ayant tué <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>; <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> fut chargé de trouver le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> sur le mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a>; comme il tardait, au moment fixé par le <a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a> <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> fit un <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> de sable que <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> consacra, et qui devint indestructible; c'est le <a href="#entry-Uraamanaatha" class="skt-link">Rāmanātha</a> <a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a> alors que le <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> himalayen est le <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha</a>, subalterne; d'autres sources disent que <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> fit la consécration d'un <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> de terre [<a href="#entry-paarthiva" class="skt-link">pārthiva</a>] avant son départ pour <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>, afin d'obtenir la bénédiction de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamaliilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmalīlā</i> [<a href="#entry-liilaa" class="skt-link">līlā</a>] <a href="#entry-raamaliilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. représentation des exploits du dieu <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> et de son combat contre <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>; elle est jouée lors du festival de <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">navarātra</a> (<a href="#entry-durgaapuujaa" class="skt-link">durgāpūjā</a> au Bengale).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamanaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmanātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-raamanaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Rāmanātha, <a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">jyotirliṅga</a> de <a href="#entry-Uraamezvara" class="skt-link">Rāmeśvara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-raamalifgezvara" class="skt-link">rāmaliṅgeśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamanavamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmanavamī</i> [<a href="#entry-navamii" class="skt-link">navamī</a>] <a href="#entry-raamanavamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. fête anniversaire de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, le 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour [<a href="#entry-tithi" class="skt-link">tithi</a>] du mois <a href="#entry-caitra" class="skt-link">caitra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamapaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmapāla</i> [<a href="#entry-paala" class="skt-link">pāla</a>] <a href="#entry-raamapaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Rāmapāla du <a href="#entry-Upu_n_dravardhana" class="skt-link">Puṇḍravardhana</a> (Bengale) (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); <a href="#entry-Usandhyaakaranandii" class="skt-link">Sandhyākaranandī</a> écrivit son panégyrique <a href="#entry-Uraamapaalacarita" class="skt-link">Rāmapālacarita</a>; il fonda le monastère bd. <a href="#entry-Ujagaddala" class="skt-link">Jagaddala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamapaalacarita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmapālacarita</i> [<a href="#entry-carita" class="skt-link">carita</a>] <a href="#entry-raamapaalacarita" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Rāmapālacarita, éloge du roi <a href="#entry-Uraamapaala" class="skt-link">Rāmapāla</a>; c'est une lecture du poème à double sens <a href="#entry-Uraamacarita_1" class="skt-link">Rāmacarita_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-raamapaala" class="skt-link">rāmapāla</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamaraajya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmarājya</i> [<a href="#entry-raajya" class="skt-link">rājya</a>] <a href="#entry-raamaraajya" class="skt-link">n.</a> mod. gouvernement intègre, justice sociale.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamasetu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmasetu</i> [<a href="#entry-setu" class="skt-link">setu</a>] <a href="#entry-raamasetu" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. ligne de rochers formant une jetée naturelle entre Ceylan et l'Inde (connue sous les anglais comme Adam's Bridge) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] c'est par ce pont, construit par les singes, que <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> envahit <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamataapin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmatāpin</i> [<a href="#entry-taapin" class="skt-link">tāpin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raamataapin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raamataapin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raamataapinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rāmatāpinī</i> soc. renonçant de dévot à <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> — <a href="#entry-raamataapinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rāmatāpinī</i> lit. np. de la leçon Rāmatāpinyupaniṣad.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamezvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmeśvara</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-raamezvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de l'île de Rāmeśvara, au sud du Tamil Nadu, important lieu de pélerinage; cf. <a href="#entry-Uraamalifgezvara" class="skt-link">Rāmaliṅgeśvara</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. temple de Rāmeśvara_1 à <a href="#entry-Ubhuvanezvara" class="skt-link">Bhuvaneśvara</a> (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamopaakhyaana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāmopākhyāna</i> [<a href="#entry-upaakhyaana" class="skt-link">upākhyāna</a>] <a href="#entry-raamopaakhyaana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Rāmopākhyāna, «Geste de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>», contée par <a href="#entry-Umaarka_n_deya" class="skt-link">Mārkaṇḍeya</a> à <a href="#entry-Uyudhi_s_thira" class="skt-link">Yudhiṣṭhira</a> au 3 ème livre du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a><br> <a href="#entry-raama" class="skt-link">rāma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raamyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">राम्या</span> <i>rāmyā</i> [ca. pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] <a href="#entry-raamyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रास</span> <i>rāsa</i> <a href="#entry-raasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> nom d'une ronde, not. celle dansée par <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> et les bouvières [<a href="#entry-gopii" class="skt-link">gopī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raasaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāsāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-aayana" class="skt-link">āyana</a>] <a href="#entry-raasaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. vermifuge; remède en gén. — <a href="#entry-raasaayanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rāsāyanī</i> méd. vaisseau de fluide corporel; veine, etc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raasaayanatantra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāsāyanatantra</i> [<a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>] <a href="#entry-raasaayanatantra" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. la science des élixirs, une section de l'<a href="#entry-Uaayurveda" class="skt-link">Āyurveda</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-raasaayana" class="skt-link">rāsāyana</a> <br> <a href="#entry-raasa" class="skt-link">rāsa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raasabha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रासभ</span> <i>rāsabha</i> <a href="#entry-raasabha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raasabhii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rāsabhī</i> natu. zoo. âne; syn. <a href="#entry-khara" class="skt-link">khara</a>, <a href="#entry-gardabha" class="skt-link">gardabha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raasaliilaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāsalīlā</i> [<a href="#entry-liilaa" class="skt-link">līlā</a>] <a href="#entry-raasaliilaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> jeu érotique, not. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> avec les bouvières [<a href="#entry-gopii" class="skt-link">gopī</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raasama_n_dala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāsamaṇḍala</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_n_dala" class="skt-link">maṇḍala</a>] <a href="#entry-raasama_n_dala" class="skt-link">n.</a> cercle où l'on danse le <a href="#entry-raasa" class="skt-link">rāsa</a> et on l'on joue le <a href="#entry-raasaliilaa" class="skt-link">rāsalīlā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रात</span> <i>rāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-raa_1" class="skt-link">rā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rātā</i> donné, accordé, conféré — ifc. donné par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">राति</span> <i>rāti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-raa_1" class="skt-link">rā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raati" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raati" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raati" class="skt-link">f.</a> généreux; favorable; gracieux, bienveillant — <a href="#entry-raati" class="skt-link">f.</a> don, faveur; grâce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raatra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रात्र</span> <i>rātra</i> ifc. de <a href="#entry-raatri" class="skt-link">rātri</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raatri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रात्रि</span> <i>rātri</i> <a href="#entry-raatri" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>rātrī</i> nuit cosmique — <a href="#entry-raatra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>rātra</i> ifc. nuit de.<br> <i>rātriṃ</i> <i>divam</i> adv. jour et nuit.<br> <i>rātriyam</i> adv. de nuit.<br> <i>navarātra</i> festival des 9 nuits de <a href="#entry-Udurgaa" class="skt-link">Durgā</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-navaraatra" class="skt-link">navarātra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raatribhojana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rātribhojana</i> [<a href="#entry-bhojana" class="skt-link">bhojana</a>] <a href="#entry-raatribhojana" class="skt-link">n.</a> repas de nuit.<br> <a href="#entry-raatri" class="skt-link">rātri</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raatrika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रात्रिक</span> <i>rātrika</i> [<a href="#entry-raatri" class="skt-link">rātri</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raatrika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raatrika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raatrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rātrikā</i> nocturne — cl. après num. qui dure tant de jours — <a href="#entry-raatrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rātrikā</i> nuit.<br> <i>pañcarātrika</i> qui dure 5 jours.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">राव</span> <i>rāva</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-rava" class="skt-link">rava</a>] <a href="#entry-raava" class="skt-link">m.</a> cri, bruit perçant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raava_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रावण</span> <i>rāvaṇa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-rava_na" class="skt-link">ravaṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raava_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raava_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raava_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rāvaṇā</i> qui crie — <a href="#entry-raava_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Rāvaṇa «Hurleur», brâhmane fils de <a href="#entry-Uvizravaa" class="skt-link">Viśravā</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ukezinii_2" class="skt-link">Keśinī_2</a>, roi-vampire [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>, chef du <a href="#entry-rak_sas" class="skt-link">rakṣas</a>; par ses austérités [<a href="#entry-tapas" class="skt-link">tapas</a>] il obtint de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> de ne pouvoir être tué ni par les dieux [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>], ni par les génies [<a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a>], ni par les trolls [<a href="#entry-yak_sa" class="skt-link">yakṣa</a>], ni par les ogres [<a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>]; il devint un tyran impie, et voulut dominer l'Univers; il se rendit au mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a> pour combattre les <a href="#entry-yak_saas" class="skt-link">yakṣās</a> de son demi-frère aîné <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a>; il assomma ce dernier, et lui déroba son char volant <a href="#entry-Upu_spaka" class="skt-link">Puṣpaka</a>; arrivé près de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, il se moqua de <a href="#entry-Unandii" class="skt-link">Nandī</a> venu l'arrêter, et qu'il traita de singe, provoquant sa malédiction qu'il périsse par les singes; il étreignit le mont <a href="#entry-Ukailaasa" class="skt-link">Kailāsa</a> qu'il fit trembler, espérant le ramener à <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a> comme autel pour sa mère agée, et même <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a> prit peur; <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> avec son orteil lui écrasa les bras pendant 1000 ans, qu'il passa à pleurer et à le supplier; alors <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> lui pardonna, lui donna un surcroît de vie et l'épée <a href="#entry-Ucandrahaasa" class="skt-link">Candrahāsa</a>; il rencontra dans les bois la belle <a href="#entry-Uvedavatii" class="skt-link">Vedavatī</a>, promise à <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a>, vivant en ascète; il la profana en touchant ses cheveux, et elle se jeta dans le feu en faisant vœu de renaître au <a href="#entry-tretaayuga" class="skt-link">tretāyuga</a> comme <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> pour provoquer sa perte; il profana le sacrifice du roi <a href="#entry-Umarutta" class="skt-link">Marutta</a>, où étaient invités les dieux, et où officiait <a href="#entry-Usa_mvarta" class="skt-link">Saṃvarta</a>; les dieux se cachèrent dans les matrices des animaux pour s'abriter; il combattit ensuite tous les rois; <a href="#entry-Upuruuravaa" class="skt-link">Purūravā</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugaya" class="skt-link">Gaya</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugaadhi" class="skt-link">Gādhi</a>, <a href="#entry-Usuratha" class="skt-link">Suratha</a>, <a href="#entry-Udu_syanta" class="skt-link">Duṣyanta</a> s'avouèrent vaincus; <a href="#entry-Uanara_nya_1" class="skt-link">Anaraṇya_1</a>, roi d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a>, le combattit en vain, et périt en le maudissant d'être vaincu par son descendant <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; <a href="#entry-Unaarada" class="skt-link">Nārada</a> lui remontra que tuer les hommes était indigne de lui, et qu'il devait plutôt se mesurer à <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>; il descendit dans les enfers combattre <a href="#entry-Uyama" class="skt-link">Yama</a>, <a href="#entry-Um_rtyu" class="skt-link">Mṛtyu</a> et leurs hordes de démons tourmenteurs, mais <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> s'interposa, car sa parole ne pouvait être mise en doute; <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> alla ensuite à <a href="#entry-Ubhogavatii" class="skt-link">Bhogavatī</a> soumettre les <a href="#entry-naagaas" class="skt-link">nāgās</a>, puis à <a href="#entry-Uma_nimatii" class="skt-link">Maṇimatī</a> combattre les <a href="#entry-nivaatakavacaas" class="skt-link">nivātakavacās</a>, qui firent la paix avec lui après une longue guerre; il alla ensuite au royaume des eaux combattre l'armée de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>; puis il rentra à <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>, enlevant des femmes et pillant tout sur son passage; il attaqua alors les dieux; il enleva et viola <a href="#entry-Urambhaa" class="skt-link">Rambhā</a>, la plus belle femme du paradis d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, bien qu'épouse de son neveu <a href="#entry-Unalakuubara" class="skt-link">Nalakūbara</a>; ce dernier le maudit de ne pouvoir séduire une femme non consentante, sous peine que sa tête éclate en sept morceaux; son fils <a href="#entry-Umeghanaada" class="skt-link">Meghanāda</a> soumit <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, et devint <a href="#entry-Uindrajit" class="skt-link">Indrajit</a>; <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> vint demander clémence, et <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> fut libéré, en contrepartie de l'immunité d'<a href="#entry-Uindrajit" class="skt-link">Indrajit</a>; il combattit <a href="#entry-Uarjuna_1" class="skt-link">Arjuna_1</a> <a href="#entry-Ukaartaviirya" class="skt-link">Kārtavīrya</a>, mais fut fait prisonnier de ses 1000 bras, et ne fut libéré que sur l'intervention de son aîeul <a href="#entry-Upulastya" class="skt-link">Pulastya</a>; il attaqua le roi des singes <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a>, occupé à ses rites du soir, mais celui-ci le saisit et l'attacha à sa ceinture, en faisant le tour des quatre océans; puis <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a> le libéra, et l'emmena à <a href="#entry-Uki_skindhaa" class="skt-link">Kiṣkindhā</a> où il accepta son offre d'alliance; lors de l'exil de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> à la forêt, grâce à la ruse de <a href="#entry-Umaariica" class="skt-link">Mārīca</a> métamorphosé en daim d'or [<a href="#entry-hemam_rga" class="skt-link">hemamṛga</a>] pour éloigner <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> enleva son épouse <a href="#entry-Usiitaa" class="skt-link">Sītā</a> qu'il emmena prisonnière à <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>; il enferma <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> dans une citerne pour échapper à son influence, mais <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a> l'en délivra, et d'un regard <a href="#entry-Uzani" class="skt-link">Śani</a> noircit son palais d'or; il fut vaincu et tué par <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> aidé de <a href="#entry-Uhanumaan" class="skt-link">Hanumān</a>, au terme d'une longue guerre où les singes et les ours étaient leurs alliés; il a un corps monstrueux, avec dix têtes [<a href="#entry-Udazagriiva" class="skt-link">Daśagrīva</a>] et vingt bras; on le représente parfois avec une onzième tête d'âne symbolisant sa folie; sa première reine est <a href="#entry-Umandodarii" class="skt-link">Mandodarī</a>, qui lui donna pour fils <a href="#entry-Umeghanaada" class="skt-link">Meghanāda</a>-<a href="#entry-Uindrajit" class="skt-link">Indrajit</a>, <a href="#entry-Uatikaaya" class="skt-link">Atikāya</a> et <a href="#entry-Uak_sakumaara" class="skt-link">Akṣakumāra</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uduu_sa_na" class="skt-link">Dūṣaṇa</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzuurpa_nakhaa" class="skt-link">Śūrpaṇakhā</a>, <a href="#entry-brahmaastra" class="skt-link">brahmāstra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raava_nahrada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāvaṇahrada</i> [<a href="#entry-hrada" class="skt-link">hrada</a>] <a href="#entry-raava_nahrada" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. du Rāvaṇahrada, lac de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, dans l'<a href="#entry-Uhimaalaya" class="skt-link">Himālaya</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uanavatapta" class="skt-link">Anavatapta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-raava_na" class="skt-link">rāvaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raava_navadha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāvaṇavadha</i> [<a href="#entry-vadha" class="skt-link">vadha</a>] <a href="#entry-raava_navadha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rāvaṇavadha «Défaite de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>», <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ti" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭi</a> (7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_tikaavya" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭikāvya</a>, ce poème sert de recueil de composition grammaticale selon <a href="#entry-Upaa_nini" class="skt-link">Pāṇini</a> et de compendium de figures de style [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>], et appartient donc au genre <a href="#entry-zaastrakaavya" class="skt-link">śāstrakāvya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raava_navaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāvaṇavaha</i> [<a href="#entry-vaha" class="skt-link">vaha</a>] <a href="#entry-raava_navaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rāvaṇavaha, poème sur la geste de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> en <a href="#entry-praak_rta" class="skt-link">prākṛta</a> attribué à <a href="#entry-Upravarasena_1" class="skt-link">Pravarasena_1</a>; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Usetubandha" class="skt-link">Setubandha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raava_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रावणि</span> <i>rāvaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-raava_na" class="skt-link">rāvaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-raava_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Rāvaṇi «fils de <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>», à la voix de tonnerre [<a href="#entry-Umeghanaada" class="skt-link">Meghanāda</a>]; il soumit <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>, et devint ainsi <a href="#entry-Uindrajit" class="skt-link">Indrajit</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">राय</span> <i>rāya</i> [pkt. <a href="#entry-raajan" class="skt-link">rājan</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-raaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. Rāya, titre royal moyen-indien; cf. <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nadeva" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇadeva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raayacuuru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रायचूरु</span> <i>rāyacūru</i> <a href="#entry-raayacuuru" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. de la ville-citadelle de Rāyacūru (mod. Raichur), au Nord-Est du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>, à la frontière du pays <a href="#entry-Uandhra" class="skt-link">Andhra</a>; elle contrôle la riche plaine du doab de confluence de la <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_naa" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇā</a> et de la <a href="#entry-Utufgabhadraa" class="skt-link">Tuṅgabhadrā</a>; le district fit partie des possessions des <a href="#entry-Ucaalukyaas" class="skt-link">Cālukyās</a> de Badāmi, puis des <a href="#entry-Uraa_s_trakuu_taas" class="skt-link">Rāṣṭrakūṭās</a>, puis des <a href="#entry-Ucaalukyaas" class="skt-link">Cālukyās</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukalyaa_nii" class="skt-link">Kalyāṇī</a>; son fort fut construit par <a href="#entry-Urudraambaa" class="skt-link">Rudrāmbā</a>, reine des <a href="#entry-Ukaakatiiyaas" class="skt-link">Kākatīyās</a>, en 1294; la ville fut mise à sac par les troupes du Sultanat de Delhi en 1312; elle fut conquise par le roi <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_nadeva" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇadeva</a> <a href="#entry-Uraaya" class="skt-link">Rāya</a> de <a href="#entry-Uvijayanagara" class="skt-link">Vijayanagara</a> en 1520, et reprise par le Sultanat de Bījāpur après la bataille de Tālīkota en 1565; elle fut capturée par Aurangzeb en 1686, puis passa au Nizam d'Hyderabad en 1724.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रायस्</span> <i>rāyas</i> [g. <a href="#entry-rai" class="skt-link">rai</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raayaspo_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāyaspoṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-po_sa" class="skt-link">poṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-raayaspo_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> augmentation de la richesse, bonne fortune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a> «qui fait prospérer».<br> <a href="#entry-raayas" class="skt-link">rāyas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raazi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">राशि</span> <i>rāśi</i> <a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">m.</a> amas, monceau, tas, pile; masse, quantité; groupe, multitude; ensemble <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. groupe d'étoiles indiquant un signe du zodiaque (grec); il y en a 12, associés aux constellations: <a href="#entry-me_sa" class="skt-link">meṣa</a> (Bélier), <a href="#entry-v_r_sa" class="skt-link">vṛṣa</a> (Taureau), <a href="#entry-mithuna" class="skt-link">mithuna</a> (Gémeaux), <a href="#entry-karka_ta" class="skt-link">karkaṭa</a> (Cancer), <a href="#entry-si_mha" class="skt-link">siṃha</a> (Lion), <a href="#entry-kanyaa" class="skt-link">kanyā</a> (Vierge), <a href="#entry-tulaa" class="skt-link">tulā</a> ou <a href="#entry-va_nija" class="skt-link">vaṇija</a> (Balance), <a href="#entry-v_rzcika" class="skt-link">vṛścika</a> (Scorpion), <a href="#entry-dhanus" class="skt-link">dhanus</a> (Sagittaire), <a href="#entry-makara" class="skt-link">makara</a> (Capricorne), <a href="#entry-kumbha" class="skt-link">kumbha</a> (Verseau) et <a href="#entry-miina" class="skt-link">mīna</a> (Poisson) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. par ext. angle ou arc de 30° <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. nombre; résultat d'une opération arithmétique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raazicakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāśicakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-raazicakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. cercle du zodiaque.<br> <a href="#entry-raazi" class="skt-link">rāśi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raazigata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rāśigata</i> [<a href="#entry-gata" class="skt-link">gata</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raazigata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raazigata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raazigataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rāśigatā</i> empilé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. calculé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rabdha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रब्ध</span> <i>rabdha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rabh" class="skt-link">rabh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rabdha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rabdha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rabdhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rabdhā</i> saisi, étreint.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rabh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रभ्</span> <i>rabh</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-labh" class="skt-link">labh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(rabhate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rabdha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> s'emparer de, saisir; étreindre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> désirer ardemment — dés. md. <i>(ripsate)</i> désirer saisir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rabhas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रभस्</span> <i>rabhas</i> [<a href="#entry-rabh" class="skt-link">rabh</a>] <a href="#entry-rabhas" class="skt-link">n.</a> violence, impétuosité; zèle, ardeur; force, énergie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rabhasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रभस</span> <i>rabhasa</i> [<a href="#entry-rabhas" class="skt-link">rabhas</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rabhasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rabhasa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rabhasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rabhasā</i> violent, impétueux, rapide; sauvage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fort puissant (se dit not. du <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>) — ifc. désireux de, empressé à — <a href="#entry-rabhasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> impétuosité, véhémence; hâte, zèle, passion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rac">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रच्</span> <i>rac</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(racayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>racita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> former, arranger, préparer; façonner, construire; faire faire ⟨2 acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> exécuter, composer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-racana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रचन</span> <i>racana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rac" class="skt-link">rac</a>] <a href="#entry-racana" class="skt-link">n.</a> préparation, composition; mise en œuvre — <a href="#entry-racanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>racanā</i> préparation, composition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> œuvre littéraire; style <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> production, fabrication; exécution <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> port (d'un vêtement); mise en formation (d'une troupe); préparation de l'empennage (d'une flèche); apprêt (d'une coiffure); fabrication (d'une guirlande de fleurs, d'un bâtiment, etc.) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. véd. np. de Racanā «Élaboration», épouse de <a href="#entry-Utva_s_taa" class="skt-link">Tvaṣṭā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-racita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रचित</span> <i>racita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rac" class="skt-link">rac</a>] a. <a href="#entry-racita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-racita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-racitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>racitā</i> construit, formé; arrangé, préparé; fait de, muni de ⟨i.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रद्</span> <i>rad</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(radati)</i> gratter, griffer; mordre, mâcher; creuser; not. creuser un canal; ouvrir un chemin <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>rodo</i>; ang. rat; fr. rat.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रद</span> <i>rada</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rad" class="skt-link">rad</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rada" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rada" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-radaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>radā</i> ifc. qui gratte, qui griffe; qui mord — <a href="#entry-rada" class="skt-link">m.</a> acte de griffer ou de mordre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. par ext. le nombre 32 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défense d'éléphant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-radana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रदन</span> <i>radana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rad" class="skt-link">rad</a>] <a href="#entry-radana" class="skt-link">m.</a> dent; dentition — <a href="#entry-radana" class="skt-link">n.</a> griffure, morsure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-radanikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>radanikā</i> [<a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] <a href="#entry-radanikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la servante Radanikā «aux belles dents» dans la <a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-radana" class="skt-link">radana</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रद्ध</span> <i>raddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-radh" class="skt-link">radh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raddhā</i> maîtrisé, vaincu <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> blessé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-radh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रध्</span> <i>radh</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(radhyati)</i> être asservi ou vaincu; arriver à maturité — ca. <i>(randhayati)</i> tourmenter; détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-radhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रधित</span> <i>radhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-radh" class="skt-link">radh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-radhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-radhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-radhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>radhitā</i> blessé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रङ्ग</span> <i>raṅga</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] <a href="#entry-rafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> coloration, teinte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. nasalisation (d'une voyelle) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> scène, estrade, théâtre; assemblée, audience <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. lustre, couleur d'une pierre précieuse associée à l'influence d'un astre [<a href="#entry-graha" class="skt-link">graha</a>]; cf. <a href="#entry-navarafga" class="skt-link">navaraṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgaavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raṅgāvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">āvali</a>] <a href="#entry-rafgaavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. rangée de dessins colorés sur le sol devant une maison, not. à l'occasion d'un festival comme <a href="#entry-Udiipaavali" class="skt-link">Dīpāvali</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>raṅgoli</i>.<br> <a href="#entry-rafga" class="skt-link">raṅga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgadaivata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raṅgadaivata</i> [<a href="#entry-daivata" class="skt-link">daivata</a>] <a href="#entry-rafgadaivata" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. [théâtre] divinité de la scène.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgadaivatapuujana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raṅgadaivatapūjana</i> [<a href="#entry-puujana" class="skt-link">pūjana</a>] <a href="#entry-rafgadaivatapuujana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. [«hommage aux divinités de la scène»] cérémonie rituelle d'offrande précédant une représentation théâtrale.<br> <a href="#entry-rafgadaivata" class="skt-link">raṅgadaivata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafganaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raṅganātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-rafganaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Raṅganātha «Protecteur de l'assemblée (des fidèles)», forme de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> adorée not. à <a href="#entry-Uzriirafga" class="skt-link">Śrīraṅga</a>, à <a href="#entry-Uzriirafgapa_t_tana" class="skt-link">Śrīraṅgapaṭṭana</a> et à Śivanāsamudrā; cf. <a href="#entry-Urafgazaayii" class="skt-link">Raṅgaśāyī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgapa_ncamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raṅgapañcamī</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncamii" class="skt-link">pañcamī</a>] <a href="#entry-rafgapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour du festival de <a href="#entry-holii" class="skt-link">holī</a> au <a href="#entry-Umahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Mahārāṣṭra</a>, où l'on célèbre les couleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgazaayin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raṅgaśāyin</i> [<a href="#entry-zaayin" class="skt-link">śāyin</a>] <a href="#entry-rafgazaayin" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Raṅgaśāyī «Dieu des couleurs reposant (sur <a href="#entry-Uze_sa" class="skt-link">Śeṣa</a>)», idole principale de <a href="#entry-Urafganaatha" class="skt-link">Raṅganātha</a>; il est représenté allongé face au Sud.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रङ्गिन्</span> <i>raṅgin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rafgin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rafgin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rafgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raṅgiṇī</i> qui colore — <a href="#entry-rafgin" class="skt-link">m.</a> colorant; teinture.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafgita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रङ्गित</span> <i>raṅgita</i> [<a href="#entry-rafga" class="skt-link">raṅga</a><a href="#entry--ita" class="skt-link">-ita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rafgita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rafgita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rafgitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raṅgitā</i> coloré; joli; beau.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rafku">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रङ्कु</span> <i>raṅku</i> <a href="#entry-rafku" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. espèce de cerf ou d'antilope.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raghu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रघु</span> <i>raghu</i> [<a href="#entry-ra_mh" class="skt-link">raṃh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raghu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raghu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raghvii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raghvī</i> rapide, léger, agile — <a href="#entry-raghu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Raghu «Agile», roi de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>], fils de <a href="#entry-Udiliipa_2" class="skt-link">Dilīpa_2</a> et <a href="#entry-Usudak_si_naa" class="skt-link">Sudakṣiṇā</a>; il est père d'<a href="#entry-Uaja" class="skt-link">Aja</a>, arrière grand-père de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>; il avait une auréole [<a href="#entry-prabhaama_n_dala" class="skt-link">prabhāmaṇḍala</a>] en forme de lotus au dessus de la tête; [<a href="#entry-Uraghuva_mza" class="skt-link">Raghuvaṃśa</a>] il conquit l'Univers, et paya son <a href="#entry-guru" class="skt-link">guru</a> par l'or que <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> avait fait tomber en pluie dans ses coffres; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukautsa" class="skt-link">Kautsa</a>; ou bien [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] il est dit fils de <a href="#entry-Ukakutstha" class="skt-link">Kakutstha</a> et ancêtre de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> par son fils <a href="#entry-Uprav_rddha" class="skt-link">Pravṛddha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raghunaatha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raghunātha</i> [<a href="#entry-naatha" class="skt-link">nātha</a>] <a href="#entry-raghunaatha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Raghunātha «Seigneur de (la race de) <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a> comme souverain d'<a href="#entry-Uayodhyaa" class="skt-link">Ayodhyā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. du grand temple de <a href="#entry-Uraghunaatha" class="skt-link">Raghunātha</a> à Jammu (19⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Raghunātha_1 <a href="#entry-Uziroma_ni" class="skt-link">Śiromaṇi</a> (1475–~1550), principal logicien bengali du <a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>; enfant prodige, borgne, et arrogant, il fut l'élève de <a href="#entry-Uvaasudeva_3" class="skt-link">Vāsudeva_3</a> <a href="#entry-Usaarvabhauma_1" class="skt-link">Sārvabhauma_1</a> puis de <a href="#entry-Upak_sadhara" class="skt-link">Pakṣadhara</a> à <a href="#entry-Umithilaa" class="skt-link">Mithilā</a>; il s'établit ensuite à <a href="#entry-Unavadviipa" class="skt-link">Navadvīpa</a>, et forma de nombreux élèves; on lui doit not. la <a href="#entry-Ugu_nakira_naavaliiprakaazadiidhiti" class="skt-link">Guṇakiraṇāvalīprakāśadīdhiti</a>, la <a href="#entry-Utattvacintaama_nidiidhiti" class="skt-link">Tattvacintāmaṇidīdhiti</a>, l'<a href="#entry-Uaakhyaatavaada" class="skt-link">Ākhyātavāda</a>, le <a href="#entry-Una_nvaada" class="skt-link">Nañvāda</a>, le <a href="#entry-Upadaarthatattvaniruupa_na" class="skt-link">Padārthatattvanirūpaṇa</a>/<a href="#entry-Upadaarthakha_n_dana" class="skt-link">Padārthakhaṇḍana</a>, l'<a href="#entry-Uaatmatattvavivekadiidhiti" class="skt-link">Ātmatattvavivekadīdhiti</a> et la <a href="#entry-Udiidhiti" class="skt-link">Dīdhiti</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Raghunātha_2 <a href="#entry-Unaayaka" class="skt-link">Nāyaka</a>, fils d'Acyutappa, régent éclairé de Thanjavur (1600–1633); lettré, il était poète, compositeur de pièces en sanskrit et en télugu, musicien, enseignant; son maître était <a href="#entry-Ugovinda_1" class="skt-link">Govinda_1</a> <a href="#entry-Udiik_sita" class="skt-link">Dīkṣita</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de Raghunātha_3 <a href="#entry-Upa_n_dita" class="skt-link">Paṇḍita</a>, lettré marathe du 17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle, auteur du <a href="#entry-Uraajavyavahaarakoza" class="skt-link">Rājavyavahārakośa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raghunaathadaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raghunāthadāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-daasa" class="skt-link">dāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-raghunaathadaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète bengali Raghunāthadāsa «Esclave de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>», disciple de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a>, l'un des six pasteurs [<a href="#entry-gosvaamin" class="skt-link">gosvāmin</a>] de <a href="#entry-Uv_rndaavana" class="skt-link">Vṛndāvana</a>; il composa le <a href="#entry-Umuktaacarita" class="skt-link">Muktācarita</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-raghunaatha" class="skt-link">raghunātha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raghunandana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raghunandana</i> [<a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">nandana</a>] <a href="#entry-raghunandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Raghunandana «Joie de (la race de) <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a>», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uraama" class="skt-link">Rāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raghuva_mza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raghuvaṃśa</i> [<a href="#entry-va_mza" class="skt-link">vaṃśa</a>] <a href="#entry-raghuva_mza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Raghuvaṃśa «La lignée de <a href="#entry-Uraghu" class="skt-link">Raghu</a>» (Ragh.), ouvrage épique de <a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>; il est inspiré de la vie de <a href="#entry-Usamudragupta" class="skt-link">Samudragupta</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-raghu" class="skt-link">raghu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rah">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रह्</span> <i>rah</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(rahayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rahita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> quitter, abandonner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rahas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रहस्</span> <i>rahas</i> [<a href="#entry-rah" class="skt-link">rah</a>] <a href="#entry-rahas" class="skt-link">n.</a> endroit désert, retraite; solitude, secret — acc. adv. en secret — loc. <i>rahasi</i> adv. en secret.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rahasya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rahasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rahasyā</i> secret, clandestin — <a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> secret <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. doctrine ésotérique (not. <a href="#entry-aara_nyaka" class="skt-link">āraṇyaka</a> ou <a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>) — acc. <i>rahasyam</i> adv. en secret.<br> <a href="#entry-rahas" class="skt-link">rahas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rahita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रहित</span> <i>rahita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rah" class="skt-link">rah</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rahita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rahita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rahitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rahitā</i> quitté, abandonné, délaissé; solitaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> privé ou exempt de ⟨i. iic.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rahuuga_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रहूगण</span> <i>rahūgaṇa</i> <a href="#entry-rahuuga_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Rahūgaṇa, auteur d'hymnes du RV., père de <a href="#entry-Ugotama" class="skt-link">Gotama</a>, un chantre [<a href="#entry-aafgirasa" class="skt-link">āṅgirasa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. clan <a href="#entry-aafgirasa" class="skt-link">āṅgirasa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rai">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रै</span> <i>rai</i> [<a href="#entry-raa_1" class="skt-link">rā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rai" class="skt-link">m.</a> richesse — <a href="#entry-rai" class="skt-link">f.</a> id. (rare).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raibha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रैभ</span> <i>raibha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-rebha" class="skt-link">rebha</a>] <a href="#entry-raibha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. patr. des descendants de <a href="#entry-Urebha" class="skt-link">Rebha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raibhya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रैभ्य</span> <i>raibhya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-rebha" class="skt-link">rebha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-raibhya" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. clan des descendants de <a href="#entry-Urebha" class="skt-link">Rebha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raivata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रैवत</span> <i>raivata</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-revat" class="skt-link">revat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raivata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raivata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raivatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raivatī</i> riche — <a href="#entry-raivata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Raivata «le Riche», 5⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de l'un des 11 <a href="#entry-marutas" class="skt-link">marutas</a>, ou de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Raivata_1 «issu de <a href="#entry-Urevatii" class="skt-link">Revatī</a>», géant qui tourmente les jeunes enfants <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. du Mont Raivata_2, près de <a href="#entry-Ukuzasthalii" class="skt-link">Kuśasthalī</a>; <a href="#entry-Uekalavya" class="skt-link">Ekalavya</a> y habitait — <a href="#entry-raivata" class="skt-link">n.</a> épith. de divers hymnes [<a href="#entry-saaman" class="skt-link">sāman</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rajaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रजक</span> <i>rajaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] <a href="#entry-rajaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rajakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rajakī</i> blanchisseur; teinturier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> homme de basse caste.<br> <i>rajakasya</i> <i>vastraṃ</i> <i>dadāti</i> il donne un vêtement au teinturier.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rajani">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रजनि</span> <i>rajani</i> [<a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] <a href="#entry-rajani" class="skt-link">f.</a> nuit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>rajanī</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ερεβος.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rajanikara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rajanikara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rajanikara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lune.<br> <a href="#entry-rajani" class="skt-link">rajani</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rajas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रजस्</span> <i>rajas</i> <a href="#entry-rajas" class="skt-link">n.</a> atmosphère; nuées <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> poussière, impuretés, pollen <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passion, instinct, désir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> règles, impureté d'une femme menstruant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la Passion, 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ qualité [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] de la nature, essence active de la force et du désir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rajasvala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rajasvala</i> [<a href="#entry--vala" class="skt-link">-vala</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rajasvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rajasvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rajasvalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rajasvalā</i> poussiéreux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> possédant le <a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a> <a href="#entry-rajas" class="skt-link">rajas</a> — <a href="#entry-rajasvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> buffle — <a href="#entry-rajasvalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rajasvalā</i> femme ayant ses règles (donc impure) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> femme nubile (donc apte au mariage).<br> <a href="#entry-rajas" class="skt-link">rajas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rajju">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रज्जु</span> <i>rajju</i> <a href="#entry-rajju" class="skt-link">f.</a> corde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. géométrie.<br> <i>rajjum</i> <i>āsthā</i> se pendre.<br> <i>rajjusarpanyāya</i> phil. maxime de la corde et du serpent, évoquant les erreurs de perception.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्ष्</span> <i>rakṣ</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-raaj_1" class="skt-link">rāj_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(rakṣati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>rakṣiṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(rarakṣa)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(ararakṣat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rakṣita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> protéger, défendre, garder; protéger de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conserver, sauver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gouverner — ps. <i>(rakṣyate)</i> être protégé — ca. <i>(rakṣayati)</i> faire protéger — dés. <i>(rirakṣiṣati)</i> vouloir protéger.<br> <i>vayam</i> <i>rakṣāmaḥ</i> (devise de l'Indian Coast Guard) Nous protégeons.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_s_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्ष्ण</span> <i>rakṣṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-rak_s_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> protection; préservation; surveillance.<br> <i>rakṣṇaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> protéger de ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्ष</span> <i>rakṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rakṣā</i> qui protège — <a href="#entry-rak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> protection, conservation, garde — f. cf. <a href="#entry-rak_saa" class="skt-link">rakṣā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षण</span> <i>rakṣaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-rak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> protection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षा</span> <i>rakṣā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-rak_sa" class="skt-link">rakṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-rak_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> garde, défense, protection; sécurité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> garde, sentinelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<a href="#entry-rak_saabandhana" class="skt-link">rakṣābandhana</a>] soc. amulette (not. cordonnet porté au poignet).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_saabandhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rakṣābandhana</i> [<a href="#entry-bandhana" class="skt-link">bandhana</a>] <a href="#entry-rak_saabandhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. cordonnet de soie noué au poignet comme amulette de protection; les sœurs en donnent à leurs frères le jour de la pleine lune <a href="#entry-zraava_nii" class="skt-link">śrāvaṇī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>rakhī</i>.<br> <a href="#entry-rak_saa" class="skt-link">rakṣā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_saakara_n_daka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rakṣākaraṇḍaka</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_n_daka" class="skt-link">karaṇḍaka</a>] <a href="#entry-rak_saakara_n_daka" class="skt-link">n.</a> amulette en forme de petite boîte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_saka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षक</span> <i>rakṣaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rak_saka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_saka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rakṣikā</i> protecteur, gardien; garde, policier — <a href="#entry-rak_sikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rakṣikā</i> amulette, charme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_sas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षस्</span> <i>rakṣas</i> [<a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rak_sas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sas" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui garde, qui surveille — <a href="#entry-rak_sas" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. «la Garde», troupe d'esprits terribles mangeant la chair crue, dirigée par le démon <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a> — <a href="#entry-rak_sas" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>rakṣasās</i> myth. ces êtres démoniaques; cf. <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_sin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षिन्</span> <i>rakṣin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rak_sin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_si_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rakṣiṇī</i> garde, protecteur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gardien; sentinelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_sit_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षितृ</span> <i>rakṣitṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rak_sit_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sit_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sitrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rakṣitrī</i> garde, gardien, protecteur; veilleur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. divinité gardienne d'une direction [<a href="#entry-dik" class="skt-link">dik</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rak_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्षित</span> <i>rakṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rak_s" class="skt-link">rakṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rak_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rak_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rakṣitā</i> gardé, protégé, préservé.<br> <i>dharmo</i> <i>rakṣati</i> <i>rakṣitaḥ</i> [MS.] Qui protège le <a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a> en reçoit la protection.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rakta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्त</span> <i>rakta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rakta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktā</i> coloré; rouge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passionné, épris de, adonné à, charmé par ⟨i. g. loc.⟩; aimant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cher, agréable — <a href="#entry-rakta" class="skt-link">n.</a> sang <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vermillon, minium; safran; cuivre — <a href="#entry-raktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktā</i> myth. np. de la déesse tantrique Raktā.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktaafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raktaafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raktaafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raktaafgaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktāṅgā</i> <a href="#entry-raktaafgii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktāṅgī</i> qui a le corps rouge — <a href="#entry-raktaafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> oiseau de couleur rouge; cochenille <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> substance rouge; la planète Mars — <a href="#entry-raktaafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> corail <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> safran.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktaak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktākṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raktaak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raktaak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raktaak_sii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktākṣī</i> qui a les yeux injectés de sang <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> horrible, effrayant — <a href="#entry-raktaak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> pigeon (aux yeux rouges) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de Raktākṣa «Décrépit», personnifiant l'an 58 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktabiija">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktabīja</i> [<a href="#entry-biija" class="skt-link">bīja</a>] <a href="#entry-raktabiija" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Raktabīja «Que son sang régénère», monstre au service de <a href="#entry-Ukiirti" class="skt-link">Kīrti</a> et <a href="#entry-Uvaasa" class="skt-link">Vāsa</a>, qui avait le pouvoir de se recréer à partir de toute goutte de son sang; il fut tué par <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>-<a href="#entry-Ukaalii" class="skt-link">Kālī</a> qui créa les 64 sorcières [<a href="#entry-yoginii" class="skt-link">yoginī</a>] pour boire son sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रक्तक</span> <i>raktaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raktaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raktaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raktikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktikā</i> amoureux, épris de, passionné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> saignant — <a href="#entry-raktaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> vêtement rouge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> amant; joueur — <a href="#entry-raktikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktikā</i> natu. bot. <i>Abrus precatorius</i>, réglisse, liane légumineuse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sa graine, utilisée comme mesure de poids; il y en a 7 dans un <a href="#entry-maa_saka" class="skt-link">māṣaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktamarica">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktamarica</i> [<a href="#entry-marica" class="skt-link">marica</a>] <a href="#entry-raktamarica" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Capsicum annuum</i>, piment; syn. <a href="#entry-ka_tuviira" class="skt-link">kaṭuvīra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktanetra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktanetra</i> [<a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">netra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raktanetra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raktanetra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raktanetraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktanetrā</i> aux yeux rouges.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktanetrataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktanetratā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-raktanetrataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> état d'avoir les yeux rouges ou injectés de sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktanetratva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktanetratva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-raktanetratva" class="skt-link">n.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-raktanetra" class="skt-link">raktanetra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktapa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_1" class="skt-link">pa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raktapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raktapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raktapii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raktapī</i> qui boit du sang — <a href="#entry-raktapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de <a href="#entry-raak_sasa" class="skt-link">rākṣasa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raktotpala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raktotpala</i> [<a href="#entry-utpala" class="skt-link">utpala</a>] <a href="#entry-raktotpala" class="skt-link">n.</a> lotus rouge.<br> <a href="#entry-rakta" class="skt-link">rakta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rallaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रल्लक</span> <i>rallaka</i> <a href="#entry-rallaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. espèce de cerf laineux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> couverture de laine.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ram">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रम्</span> <i>ram</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(ramati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rata</i><i>)</i> inf. <i>(rantum)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>ramaṇīya</i><i>)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] <i>(araṃsīt)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> véd. charmer, réjouir, satisfaire; not. satisfaire sexuellement — pr. md. <i>(ramate)</i> se reposer, rester auprès de ⟨loc. dat.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se réjouir, être heureux, s'amuser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se plaire à, trouver du plaisir à ⟨loc. i. inf.⟩; jouer ou faire l'amour avec ⟨i.-<a href="#entry-saha" class="skt-link">saha</a>⟩ — ca. <i>(ramayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ramita</i><i>)</i> charmer, réjouir, rendre heureux; donner du plaisir à, jouir sexuellement avec — ca. <i>(rāmayati)</i> id. — dés. md. <i>(riraṃsate)</i> désirer sexuellement <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ηρεμα.<br> <i>rantumanas</i> a. m. n. qui pense à faire l'amour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रम</span> <i>rama</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramā</i> ifc. qui charme; aimé, amant de ⟨iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui joue (dans ⟨iic.⟩ loc.) — <a href="#entry-ramaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramā</i> splendeur; fortune, opulence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Ramā «Splendide», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rama_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रमण</span> <i>ramaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rama_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rama_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rama_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramaṇī</i> qui charme, qui réjouit — <a href="#entry-rama_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> amant, époux — <a href="#entry-rama_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de réjouir; coït.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rama_niiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रमणीय</span> <i>ramaṇīya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rama_niiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rama_niiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rama_niiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramaṇīyā</i> aimable, charmant, agréable, plaisant.<br> <i>rāmaṇīyārthapratipādakaḥ</i> <i>śabdaḥ</i> <i>kāvyam</i> [<a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha_1" class="skt-link">Jagannātha_1</a>] La poésie, c'est de dire des mots dont le sens est plaisant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rama_niiyadarzana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ramaṇīyadarśana</i> [<a href="#entry-darzana" class="skt-link">darśana</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rama_niiyadarzana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rama_niiyadarzana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rama_niiyadarzanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramaṇīyadarśanā</i> d'agréable apparence.<br> <a href="#entry-rama_niiya" class="skt-link">ramaṇīya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rama_niiyataa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ramaṇīyatā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-rama_niiyataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> beauté; charme.<br> <i>kṣaṇe</i> <i>kṣaṇe</i> <i>yan</i> <i>navatām</i> <i>upaiti</i> <i>tad</i> <i>eva</i> <i>rūpam</i> <i>ramaṇīyatāyāḥ</i> [<a href="#entry-Umaagha" class="skt-link">Māgha</a>] Ce qui est nouveau à tout instant est la forme même de la beauté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rama_niiyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ramaṇīyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-rama_niiyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> beauté; charme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रम्भ</span> <i>rambha</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rabh" class="skt-link">rabh</a>] <a href="#entry-rambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> support, soutien; désir ardent; saisie, prise, enlèvement — f. cf. <a href="#entry-rambhaa" class="skt-link">rambhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rambhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रम्भा</span> <i>rambhā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-rambha" class="skt-link">rambha</a>] <a href="#entry-rambhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de la nymphe [<a href="#entry-apsaras" class="skt-link">apsaras</a>] Rambhā «Ravie», plus belle femme du paradis d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>; elle fut enlevée par <a href="#entry-Uraava_na" class="skt-link">Rāvaṇa</a>, bien qu'épouse de son neveu <a href="#entry-Unalakuubara" class="skt-link">Nalakūbara</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uazvah_rdayaa" class="skt-link">Aśvahṛdayā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ramita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रमित</span> <i>ramita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ramita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ramita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ramitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramitā</i> comblé, satisfait, rendu heureux ou joyeux — <a href="#entry-ramita" class="skt-link">n.</a> plaisir, jouissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ramya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रम्य</span> <i>ramya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ramya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ramya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ramyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramyā</i> joli, charmant, délicieux, aimable, agréable — <a href="#entry-ramya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ramya «Charmant», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaagniidhra" class="skt-link">Āgnīdhra</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uramyaka" class="skt-link">Ramyaka</a> — <a href="#entry-ramya" class="skt-link">n.</a> sperme — <a href="#entry-ramyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramyā</i> nuit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Ramyā «Charmante», fille de <a href="#entry-Umeru" class="skt-link">Meru</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uramya" class="skt-link">Ramya</a>, aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Uramyatvaa" class="skt-link">Ramyatvā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ramyadaaru_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ramyadāruṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-daaru_na" class="skt-link">dāruṇa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ramyadaaru_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ramyadaaru_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ramyadaaru_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramyadāruṇā</i> charmant et cruel à la fois.<br> <a href="#entry-ramya" class="skt-link">ramya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ramyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रम्यक</span> <i>ramyaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] <a href="#entry-ramyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ramyaka «Charmant», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaagniidhra" class="skt-link">Āgnīdhra</a>, aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uramya" class="skt-link">Ramya</a>; son épouse est <a href="#entry-Uramyaa" class="skt-link">Ramyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ramyakavar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ramyakavarṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-ramyakavar_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. l'un des 9 bassins versants [<a href="#entry-var_sa" class="skt-link">varṣa</a>] du monde.<br> <a href="#entry-ramyaka" class="skt-link">ramyaka</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ramyatva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ramyatva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-ramyatva" class="skt-link">n.</a> charme, beauté — <a href="#entry-ramyatvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ramyatvā</i> myth. np. de Ramyatvā «Charmante», fille de <a href="#entry-Umeru" class="skt-link">Meru</a>, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uramya" class="skt-link">Ramya</a>, aussi appelée <a href="#entry-Uramyaa" class="skt-link">Ramyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ran">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रन्</span> <i>ran</i> cf. <a href="#entry-ra_n" class="skt-link">raṇ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randh">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रन्ध्</span> <i>randh</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-radh" class="skt-link">radh</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(randhayati)</i> tourmenter; détruire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randhana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रन्धन</span> <i>randhana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-randh" class="skt-link">randh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-randhana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-randhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-randhanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>randhanā</i> qui détruit — <a href="#entry-randhana" class="skt-link">n.</a> destruction <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fait de cuisiner.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randhita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रन्धित</span> <i>randhita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-randh" class="skt-link">randh</a>] a. <a href="#entry-randhita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-randhita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-randhitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>randhitā</i> vaincu, détruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cuisiné, préparé (mets).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रन्ध्र</span> <i>randhra</i> [<a href="#entry-randh" class="skt-link">randh</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-randhra" class="skt-link">n.</a> ouverture, trou, fente; fissure, blessure; cavité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. ouverture du corps humain (syn. <a href="#entry-chidra" class="skt-link">chidra</a>); on en compte 9: 1 bouche, 2 yeux, 2 narines, 2 oreilles, 1 anus et 1 orifice urino-sexuel, et parfois on ajoute en 10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ la fontanelle [<a href="#entry-brahmarandhra" class="skt-link">brahmarandhra</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 9 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> narine, vagin; fontanelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> défaut, imperfection; point faible; not. gorge d'un cheval.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randhraanve_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>randhrānveṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-anve_sa_na" class="skt-link">anveṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-randhraanve_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> recherche des points faibles.<br> <a href="#entry-randhra" class="skt-link">randhra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randhragupti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>randhragupti</i> [<a href="#entry-gupti" class="skt-link">gupti</a>] <a href="#entry-randhragupti" class="skt-link">f.</a> dissimulation de ses points faibles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-randhraprahaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>randhraprahārin</i> [<a href="#entry-prahaarin" class="skt-link">prahārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-randhraprahaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-randhraprahaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-randhraprahaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>randhraprahāriṇī</i> qui attaque les points faibles (de son adversaire).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rapas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रपस्</span> <i>rapas</i> <a href="#entry-rapas" class="skt-link">n.</a> infirmité, blessure; maladie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रस</span> <i>rasa</i> <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> suc, jus, mœlle; sirop, liquide; not. jus de fruits ou de canne à sucre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [méd.] fluide du corps, serum, chyle, sperme; potion, élixir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> métal en fusion; mercure; minerai <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> goût, saveur; sensation; sauce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] du goût [<a href="#entry-jihvaa" class="skt-link">jihvā</a>]; son régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] est <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] la qualité [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] du goût <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] l'un des 6 types de saveurs: <a href="#entry-madhura" class="skt-link">madhura</a> le sucré, <a href="#entry-amla" class="skt-link">amla</a> l'acide, <a href="#entry-lava_na" class="skt-link">lavaṇa</a> le salé, <a href="#entry-ka_tuka" class="skt-link">kaṭuka</a> l'épicé, <a href="#entry-tikta" class="skt-link">tikta</a> l'amer, et <a href="#entry-ka_saaya" class="skt-link">kaṣāya</a> l'astringent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 6 <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sentiment, émotion, caractère; humeur; affection <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charme, agrément; plaisir, délice; condiment <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. [<a href="#entry-Unaa_tyazaastra" class="skt-link">Nāṭyaśāstra</a>] expérience esthétique, mode artistique évoquant une émotion; la tradition en compte 9: <a href="#entry-z_rfgaara" class="skt-link">śṛṅgāra</a> l'érotique, <a href="#entry-haasya" class="skt-link">hāsya</a> le comique, <a href="#entry-karu_na" class="skt-link">karuṇa</a> le pathétique, <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">raudra</a> la fureur, <a href="#entry-viira" class="skt-link">vīra</a> l'héroïque, <a href="#entry-bhayaanaka" class="skt-link">bhayānaka</a> l'effroyable, <a href="#entry-biibhatsa" class="skt-link">bībhatsa</a> l'odieux, <a href="#entry-adbhuta" class="skt-link">adbhuta</a> le merveilleux, et (plus tard) <a href="#entry-zaanta" class="skt-link">śānta</a> le paisible; les émotions évoquées correspondent aux 9 émotions de base [<a href="#entry-sthaayibhaava" class="skt-link">sthāyibhāva</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil.
