[Biardeau] jouissance esthétique dépersonnalisée aidant à transcender l'ego [<a href="#entry-ahafkaara" class="skt-link">ahaṃkāra</a>] par l'expérience de l'émerveillement [<a href="#entry-camatkaara" class="skt-link">camatkāra</a>] — <a href="#entry-rasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasā</i> ifc. jus de, suc de; goût de; émotion évoquée par — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(rasayati)</i> goûter, apprécier, ressentir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>ros</i>; fr. raisin.<br> <i>suputraḥ</i> <i>saptamo</i> <i>rasaḥ</i> Un bon fils est comme un 7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ délice.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसा</span> <i>rasā</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> humidité; rosée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de la rivière céleste Rasā ou Voie lactée; [RV.] dans une de ses îles se trouvait la caverne <a href="#entry-Uvala" class="skt-link">Vala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enfer; sol.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaaj_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasājñāna</i> [<a href="#entry-aj_naana" class="skt-link">ajñāna</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaaj_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. perte du sens du goût, ageusia; syn. <a href="#entry-arasaj_nataa" class="skt-link">arasajñatā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसाल</span> <i>rasāla</i> [<a href="#entry-rasaa" class="skt-link">rasā</a><a href="#entry--la" class="skt-link">-la</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> manguier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> canne à sucre — <a href="#entry-rasaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasālā</i> lait caillé avec sucre et épices <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> langue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaar_nava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasārṇava</i> [<a href="#entry-ar_nava" class="skt-link">arṇava</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaar_nava" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rasārṇava «Océan des humeurs», ouvrage d'alchimie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaasvaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasāsvāda</i> [<a href="#entry-aasvaada" class="skt-link">āsvāda</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaasvaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> action de siroter un jus <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. perception du plaisir, jouissance du <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaatala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasātala</i> [<a href="#entry-tala" class="skt-link">tala</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaatala" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. du monde [<a href="#entry-loka" class="skt-link">loka</a>] Rasātala, quatrième enfer souterrain; [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] les fils de <a href="#entry-Usagara" class="skt-link">Sagara</a> y cherchèrent le cheval de son sacrifice.<br> <a href="#entry-rasaa" class="skt-link">rasā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaayana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasāyana</i> [<a href="#entry-ayana" class="skt-link">ayana</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaayana" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a>] élixir de jouvence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> alchimie — <a href="#entry-rasaayana" class="skt-link">m.</a> alchimiste — <a href="#entry-rasaayanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasāyanī</i> méd. canal de fluide corporel; veine, artère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasabhaasa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasabhāsa</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaasa" class="skt-link">bhāsa</a>] <a href="#entry-rasabhaasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. illusion d'une émotion esthétique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasadhvani">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasadhvani</i> [<a href="#entry-dhvani" class="skt-link">dhvani</a>] <a href="#entry-rasadhvani" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. évocation d'une émotion esthétique; suggestion.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasagafgaadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasagaṅgādhara</i> [<a href="#entry-gafgaadhara" class="skt-link">gaṅgādhara</a>] <a href="#entry-rasagafgaadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rasagaṅgādhara «<a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> de l'émotion esthétique», traité de rhétorique et de poétique de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha_1" class="skt-link">Jagannātha_1</a> <a href="#entry-Upa_n_ditaraaja" class="skt-link">Paṇḍitarāja</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaj_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasajña</i> [<a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasaj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasaj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasajñā</i> qui a du goût, connaisseur en ⟨g. iic.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> familier de ⟨loc. iic.⟩ — <a href="#entry-rasaj_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> poète expert en esthétique; alchimiste expert en mercure; préparateur en pharmacie — <a href="#entry-rasaj_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasaj_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasajñā</i> langue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasakara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasakara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasakaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasakarā</i> savoureux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasama_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasamañjarī</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>] <a href="#entry-rasama_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Rasamañjarī «Bouquet d'émotion esthétique», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Ubhaanudatta" class="skt-link">Bhānudatta</a> sur l'esthétique en poésie sanskrite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसन</span> <i>rasana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <a href="#entry-rasana" class="skt-link">m.</a> salive — <a href="#entry-rasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> goût, saveur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gustation; perception — f. cf. <a href="#entry-rasanaa" class="skt-link">rasanā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसना</span> <i>rasanā</i> [f. <a href="#entry-rasana" class="skt-link">rasana</a>] <a href="#entry-rasanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> langue (organe du goût).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasaprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rasaprakāśa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaprakaazasudhaakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasaprakāśasudhākara</i> [<a href="#entry-sudhaa" class="skt-link">sudhā</a>-<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaprakaazasudhaakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rasaprakāśasudhākara, traité d'<a href="#entry-aayurveda" class="skt-link">āyurveda</a> dû à <a href="#entry-Uyazodhara_1" class="skt-link">Yaśodhara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-rasaprakaaza" class="skt-link">rasaprakāśa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaratnasamuccaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasaratnasamuccaya</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>-<a href="#entry-samuccaya" class="skt-link">samuccaya</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaratnasamuccaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rasaratnasamuccaya «Compendium sur les gemmes et les métaux», ouvrage d'alchimie du 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle traditionnellement attribué à <a href="#entry-Uvaagbha_ta" class="skt-link">Vāgbhaṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasatarafgi_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasataraṅgiṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-tarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">taraṅgiṇī</a>] <a href="#entry-rasatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Rasataraṅgiṇī «Rivière d'émotion esthétique», œuvre de <a href="#entry-Ubhaanudatta" class="skt-link">Bhānudatta</a> sur les types de caractères en poésie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasavat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasavatī</i> plein de jus, succulent; savoureux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gracieux, agréable; charmant, élégant, de bon goût; spirituel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> passionné, ému <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> humide, bien arrosé, irrigué <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-Uudbha_ta" class="skt-link">Udbhaṭa</a>] figure rhétorique du développement achevé d'un mode esthétique [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] par l'utilisation de situations dramatiques [<a href="#entry-vibhaava" class="skt-link">vibhāva</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-preyasvat" class="skt-link">preyasvat</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasavid">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasavid</i> [<a href="#entry-vid_3" class="skt-link">vid_3</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasavid" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasavid" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasavid" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui a bon goût.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasaviddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasaviddha</i> [<a href="#entry-viddha" class="skt-link">viddha</a>] <a href="#entry-rasaviddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> (alchimie) transmutation du cuivre en or par le mercure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>] transformation irréversible de l'âme en <a href="#entry-zivatva" class="skt-link">śivatva</a> par l'initiation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasavidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasavidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-rasavidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«connaissance du mercure»] alchimie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-rasazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> alchimie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसेन्द्र</span> <i>rasendra</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>-<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-rasendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> mercure [«sperme d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a>»].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasendracuu_daama_ni">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasendracūḍāmaṇi</i> [<a href="#entry-cuu_daama_ni" class="skt-link">cūḍāmaṇi</a>] <a href="#entry-rasendracuu_daama_ni" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rasendracūḍāmaṇi «Diadème du mercure», compendium d'alchimie dû à <a href="#entry-Usomadeva" class="skt-link">Somadeva</a> (12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).<br> <a href="#entry-rasendra" class="skt-link">rasendra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasika">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसिक</span> <i>rasika</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a><a href="#entry--ika" class="skt-link">-ika</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasika" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasika" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasikā</i> esthète, raffiné, qui a du goût; élégant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> amateur, adonné à, passionné de ⟨loc.⟩ — <a href="#entry-rasikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasikā</i> femme passionnée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasikajiivana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasikajīvana</i> [<a href="#entry-jiivana" class="skt-link">jīvana</a>] <a href="#entry-rasikajiivana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Rasikajīvana, anthologie de <a href="#entry-subhaa_sita" class="skt-link">subhāṣita</a> [<a href="#entry-subhaa_sitasafgraha" class="skt-link">subhāṣitasaṃgraha</a>] composé vers 1660 par <a href="#entry-Ugadaadhara_2" class="skt-link">Gadādhara_2</a> <a href="#entry-Ubha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Bhaṭṭa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-rasika" class="skt-link">rasika</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसिन्</span> <i>rasin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasinī</i> juteux, liquide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a bon goût.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसित</span> <i>rasita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasitā</i> goûté, apprécié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui a du goût <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plaqué, doré.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasoddiipana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasoddīpana</i> [<a href="#entry-uddiipana" class="skt-link">uddīpana</a>] <a href="#entry-rasoddiipana" class="skt-link">n.</a> stimulation d'un sentiment.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasoddiipanasaamarthya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rasoddīpanasāmarthya</i> [<a href="#entry-saamarthya" class="skt-link">sāmarthya</a>] <a href="#entry-rasoddiipanasaamarthya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. aptitude à stimuler un sentiment poétique.<br> <a href="#entry-rasoddiipana" class="skt-link">rasoddīpana</a> <br> <a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasona">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रसोन</span> <i>rasona</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>-<a href="#entry-uuna" class="skt-link">ūna</a>] <a href="#entry-rasona" class="skt-link">m.</a> ail [«qui a (mauvais) goût»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>rasuna</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रस्य</span> <i>rasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rasya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rasyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rasyā</i> juteux; savoureux, de bon goût — <a href="#entry-rasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> sang.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रत</span> <i>rata</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ratā</i> qui se réjouit, qui aime; réjoui, satisfait — ifc. amoureux de; qui a des rapports sexuels avec — <a href="#entry-rata" class="skt-link">n.</a> plaisir; plaisir sexuel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratabandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-ratabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> union sexuelle.<br> <a href="#entry-rata" class="skt-link">rata</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रथ</span> <i>ratha</i> [<a href="#entry-_r" class="skt-link">ṛ</a>] <a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">m.</a> chariot, char de guerre (à 2 roues), char de procession <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véhicule en gén. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> guerrier sur un char; héros <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> char, pièce corr. à la tour aux échecs [<a href="#entry-caturafgaa" class="skt-link">caturaṅgā</a>]; parfois plutôt appelée navire [<a href="#entry-naukaa" class="skt-link">naukā</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>rota</i>; all. Rad.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathaafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-afga_1" class="skt-link">aṅga_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rathaafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> partie de chariot — <a href="#entry-rathaafga" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Tadorna ferruginea</i>, canard rouge; syn. <a href="#entry-cakravaaka" class="skt-link">cakravāka</a>, <a href="#entry-cakraafga" class="skt-link">cakrāṅga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathacakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathacakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-rathacakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> roue de char.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathakaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathakāma</i> [<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rathakaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rathakaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rathakaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rathakāmā</i> qui désire un chariot — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(rathakāmyati)</i> désirer un chariot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-rathakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> charpentier, charron <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. fils d'un <a href="#entry-maahi_sya" class="skt-link">māhiṣya</a> et d'une <a href="#entry-kara_nii" class="skt-link">karaṇī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. le <a href="#entry-rathakaara" class="skt-link">rathakāra</a> était un personnage important de la cour d'un roi; il avait à sa disposition un palais permettant de recevoir les assistants d'un sacrifice de cheval [<a href="#entry-azvamedha" class="skt-link">aśvamedha</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. (par ext.) personne de caste mélangée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathakaaranyaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathakāranyāya</i> [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] <a href="#entry-rathakaaranyaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. maxime à l'appui du fait que le sens conventionnel d'un mot [<a href="#entry-ruu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">rūḍhārtha</a>] a priorité sur son sens étymologique [<a href="#entry-yaugikaartha" class="skt-link">yaugikārtha</a>]; c'est ainsi qu'un homme de caste mélangée peut être désigné comme <a href="#entry-rathakaara" class="skt-link">rathakāra</a> sans être forcément un charron, et qu'inversement un brâhmane qui construit des chars n'est pas pour autant un <a href="#entry-rathakaara" class="skt-link">rathakāra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-rathakaara" class="skt-link">rathakāra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratham">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रथम्</span> <i>ratham</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathantara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathaṃtara</i> [<a href="#entry--tara" class="skt-link">-tara</a>] <a href="#entry-ratha_mtara" class="skt-link">n.</a> véd. hymne de louange [<a href="#entry-saaman" class="skt-link">sāman</a>] particulièrement sacré, souvent associé à un hymne <a href="#entry-b_rhat" class="skt-link">bṛhat</a> — <a href="#entry-ratha_mtara" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Rathaṃtara «Meilleur véhicule» (pour les oblations), épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ratham" class="skt-link">ratham</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathapufgava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathapuṃgava</i> [<a href="#entry-pufgava" class="skt-link">puṃgava</a>] <a href="#entry-rathapu_mgava" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. commandant des chars, titre militaire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathasattama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathasattama</i> [<a href="#entry-sattama" class="skt-link">sattama</a>] <a href="#entry-rathasattama" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. d'homme «le meilleur des auriges».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathayaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathayāta</i> [<a href="#entry-yaata" class="skt-link">yāta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rathayaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rathayaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rathayaataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rathayātā</i> parti en chariot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathayaatraa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathayātrā</i> [<a href="#entry-yaatraa" class="skt-link">yātrā</a>] <a href="#entry-rathayaatraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. procession de chars; not. festival de <a href="#entry-Ujagannaatha" class="skt-link">Jagannātha</a> à <a href="#entry-Upurii" class="skt-link">Purī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रथी</span> <i>rathī</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>] <a href="#entry-rathii" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rathii" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui combat sur un char.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रथिन्</span> <i>rathin</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] <a href="#entry-rathin" class="skt-link">m.</a> guerrier (combattant sur un char).<br> <i>ātmānaṃ</i> <i>rathinaṃ</i> <i>viddhi</i> <i>śarīraṃ</i> <i>rathameva</i> <i>tu</i> [KU.] Connais toi comme le conducteur du chariot, et ton corps comme seulement le chariot.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathotsava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rathotsava</i> [<a href="#entry-utsava" class="skt-link">utsava</a>] <a href="#entry-rathotsava" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. festival de procession de chars sacrés.<br> <a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rathya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रथ्य</span> <i>rathya</i> [<a href="#entry-ratha" class="skt-link">ratha</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-rathya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cheval (de trait) — <a href="#entry-rathyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rathyā</i> route, rue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रति</span> <i>rati</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] <a href="#entry-rati" class="skt-link">f.</a> plaisir, joie; volupté, jouissance sexuelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Rati «Volupté», déesse personnifiant le Plaisir charnel, épouse de <a href="#entry-Ukaama" class="skt-link">Kāma</a>; elle s'incarna comme <a href="#entry-Umaayaavatii" class="skt-link">Māyāvatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratibhoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratibhoga</i> [<a href="#entry-bhoga_2" class="skt-link">bhoga_2</a>] <a href="#entry-ratibhoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> plaisir sexuel.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratikaama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratikāma</i> [<a href="#entry-kaama" class="skt-link">kāma</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ratikaama" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ratikaama" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ratikaamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ratikāmā</i> qui cherche le plaisir sexuel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. qualifie un démon de possession de caractère froid [<a href="#entry-saumya" class="skt-link">saumya</a>]; opp. <a href="#entry-hantukaama" class="skt-link">hantukāma</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ratimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ratimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ratimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ratimatī</i> qui a du plaisir; qui prend du plaisir à ⟨loc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> joyeux, gai; amoureux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratima_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratimañjarī</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>] <a href="#entry-ratima_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np.de la Ratimañjarī, ouvrage d'érotique [<a href="#entry-kaamazaastra" class="skt-link">kāmaśāstra</a>]; on y décrit 4 types de femmes: <a href="#entry-citri_nii" class="skt-link">citriṇī</a>, <a href="#entry-ruupi_nii" class="skt-link">rūpiṇī</a>, <a href="#entry-zafkhinii" class="skt-link">śaṅkhinī</a>, et <a href="#entry-hastinii" class="skt-link">hastinī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratirahasya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratirahasya</i> [<a href="#entry-rahasya" class="skt-link">rahasya</a>] <a href="#entry-ratirahasya" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Ratirahasya «Secrets du plaisir», œuvre d'érotique attribuée à un certain Koka ou Kokkoka (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratizaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratiśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-ratizaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. np. du Ratiśāstra «Secrets du plaisir», ouvrage d'érotique attribué à <a href="#entry-Unaagaarjuna" class="skt-link">Nāgārjuna</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-rati" class="skt-link">rati</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रत्न</span> <i>ratna</i> [<a href="#entry-raa_1" class="skt-link">rā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">n.</a> joyau, gemme, pierre précieuse; cf. <a href="#entry-mahaaratna" class="skt-link">mahāratna</a>, <a href="#entry-uparatna" class="skt-link">uparatna</a>, <a href="#entry-navaratna" class="skt-link">navaratna</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perle — ifc. le meilleur parmi.<br> <i>bhārata</i> <i>ratna</i> mod. plus haute décoration civile indienne.<br> <i>pṛthivyāṃ</i> <i>trīṇi</i> <i>ratnāni</i> <i>āpa</i> <i>annaṃ</i> <i>subhāṣitam</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>mūḍhaiḥ</i> <i>pāṣāṇakhaṇḍeṣu</i> <i>ratnasaṃkhyā</i> <i>vidhīyate</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> Il y a trois joyaux sur terre: l'eau, la nourriture et la bonne parole; ceux qui appellent joyaux des cailloux sont des imbéciles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnaakara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnākara</i> [<a href="#entry-aakara" class="skt-link">ākara</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnaakara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mine de joyaux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> océan <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. np. de Ratnākara «Adamantin», épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-raajaanaka" class="skt-link">rājānaka</a> Ratnākara_1, poète du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> du 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Uharavijaya" class="skt-link">Haravijaya</a> <a href="#entry-mahaakaavya" class="skt-link">mahākāvya</a> et la <a href="#entry-Uvakroktipa_ncaazikaa" class="skt-link">Vakroktipañcāśikā</a> — ifc. lit. titre d'ouvrages — <a href="#entry-ratnaakaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ratnākarā</i> ifc. mine des trésors de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnaakarazaanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnākaraśānti</i> [<a href="#entry-zaanti" class="skt-link">śānti</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnaakarazaanti" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du bd. Ratnākaraśānti (11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur de l'ouvrage de prosodie <a href="#entry-Uchandoratnaakara" class="skt-link">Chandoratnākara</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ratnaakara" class="skt-link">ratnākara</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnaavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-aavalii" class="skt-link">āvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnaavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> collier de perles <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Ratnāvalī «Rivière de diamants», une épouse de <a href="#entry-Ucitrasena" class="skt-link">Citrasena</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Ugaalava" class="skt-link">Gālava</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Ratnāvalī_1, comédie de cour de <a href="#entry-Uhar_sa" class="skt-link">Harṣa</a>, dans le style <a href="#entry-naa_tikaa" class="skt-link">nāṭikā</a>; elle relate un épisode galant de la vie du roi <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de Ratnāvalī_2, héroïne du poème éponyme, fille du roi de Ceylan; le ministre <a href="#entry-Uyaugandharaaya_na" class="skt-link">Yaugaṃdharāyaṇa</a>, ayant appris par une prophétie que l'homme qu'elle épousera deviendra le maître du monde, persuada son père d'accorder sa main au roi <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a>; elle fit naufrage, et fut recueillie dans sa cour comme demoiselle d'honneur de la reine <a href="#entry-Uvaasavadattaa_2" class="skt-link">Vāsavadattā_2</a> sous le nom de <a href="#entry-Usaagarikaa" class="skt-link">Sāgarikā</a>; à la fête du printemps, elle tombe amoureuse du roi <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a>, se fait surprendre par la Reine en sa compagnie, et après de nombreuses péripéties pourra l'épouser.<br> <a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnabhaa_n_daagaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnabhāṇḍāgāra</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaa_n_daagaara" class="skt-link">bhāṇḍāgāra</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnabhaa_n_daagaara" class="skt-link">n.</a> trésor de joyaux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. anthologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnacakra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnacakra</i> [<a href="#entry-cakra" class="skt-link">cakra</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnacakra" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«roue joyau»] roue de la souveraineté, attribut du monarque universel [<a href="#entry-cakravartin" class="skt-link">cakravartin</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. not. roue de la Loi, symbolisant l'enseignement de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-dharmacakra" class="skt-link">dharmacakra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnadviipa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnadvīpa</i> [<a href="#entry-dviipa" class="skt-link">dvīpa</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnadviipa" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de Ratnadvīpa, épith. de Ceylan «l'île aux joyaux».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnagarbha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnagarbha</i> [<a href="#entry-garbha" class="skt-link">garbha</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnagarbha" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Ratnagarbha, épith. de <a href="#entry-Ukubera" class="skt-link">Kubera</a> «Ventre de joyaux».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnagiri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnagiri</i> [<a href="#entry-giri" class="skt-link">giri</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnagiri" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. np. de la colline Ratnagiri en Orissa; cf. <a href="#entry-Upu_spagiri" class="skt-link">Puṣpagiri</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnagotravibhaaga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnagotravibhāga</i> [<a href="#entry-gotra" class="skt-link">gotra</a>-<a href="#entry-vibhaaga" class="skt-link">vibhāga</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnagotravibhaaga" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. du Ratnagotravibhāga, qui expose la doctrine <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a> du <a href="#entry-tathaagatagarbha" class="skt-link">tathāgatagarbha</a>; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uuttaratantra" class="skt-link">Uttaratantra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnagu_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnaguṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnagu_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. du Ratnaguṇa ou Ratnaguṇasaṃcayagātha, texte de 302 vers précurseur de l'<a href="#entry-Ua_s_tasaahasrikaa" class="skt-link">Aṣṭasāhasrikā</a> <a href="#entry-Upraj_naapaaramitaa" class="skt-link">Prajñāpāramitā</a> en dialecte hybride bd. du sanskrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnahavis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnahavis</i> [<a href="#entry-havis" class="skt-link">havis</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnahavis" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. oblation lors du sacre [<a href="#entry-raajasuuya" class="skt-link">rājasūya</a>] concernant les trésors du roi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnakha_n_dama_njuu_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnakhaṇḍamañjūṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_dama_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">khaṇḍamañjūṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnakha_n_dama_njuu_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. anthologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnakiirti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnakīrti</i> [<a href="#entry-kiirti" class="skt-link">kīrti</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnakiirti" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du philosophe bd. Ratnakīrti (1⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ moitié du 11⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), apôtre de la doctrine de l'exclusion [<a href="#entry-apohavaada" class="skt-link">apohavāda</a>]; on lui doit not. l'<a href="#entry-Uapohasiddhi" class="skt-link">Apohasiddhi</a> et le <a href="#entry-Usantaanaantaraduu_sa_na" class="skt-link">Santānāntaradūṣaṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnakoza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnakośa</i> [<a href="#entry-koza" class="skt-link">kośa</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnakoza" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [«recueil de perles»] anthologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnakuu_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnakūṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-kuu_ta" class="skt-link">kūṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnakuu_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. np. du Ratnakūṭa «Multitude de joyaux», collection de 49 ouvrages de la tradition <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnapariik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnaparīkṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-pariik_saa" class="skt-link">parīkṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnapariik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. appréciation des gemmes <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. np. de la Ratnaparīkṣā «Appréciation des gemmes», traité de gemmologie [<a href="#entry-ratnazaastra" class="skt-link">ratnaśāstra</a>] de <a href="#entry-Ubuddhabha_t_ta" class="skt-link">Buddhabhaṭṭa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnaprakaaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnaprakāśa</i> [<a href="#entry-prakaaza" class="skt-link">prakāśa</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnaprakaaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. np. du Ratnaprakāśa «Éclat des joyaux», commentaire du <a href="#entry-Umahaabhaa_sya" class="skt-link">Mahābhāṣya</a> par Śivarāmendra <a href="#entry-Usarasvatii_2" class="skt-link">Sarasvatī_2</a> (17⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnapurii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnapurī</i> [<a href="#entry-purii" class="skt-link">purī</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnapurii" class="skt-link">f.</a> géo. np. de la ville de Ratnapurī «Ville des joyaux», capitale d'un petit état princier du pays <a href="#entry-Uavanti" class="skt-link">Avanti</a>-<a href="#entry-Umaalava" class="skt-link">Mālava</a>; mod. Ratlam au Madhya Pradesh.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnasabhaapati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnasabhāpati</i> [<a href="#entry-sabhaapati" class="skt-link">sabhāpati</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnasabhaapati" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. np. du Ratnasabhāpati «Joyau Seigneur du sanctuaire», image de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a> <a href="#entry-Una_taraaja" class="skt-link">Naṭarāja</a> en rubis du sanctuaire de <a href="#entry-Ucidambaram" class="skt-link">Cidambaram</a>; il est dit provenir d'un sacrifice de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnasambhava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnasaṃbhava</i> [<a href="#entry-sambhava" class="skt-link">saṃbhava</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnasa_mbhava" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Ratnasaṃbhava «Joyau né», <a href="#entry-jina" class="skt-link">jina</a> gardien du Sud dans le panthéon du <a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>, représenté en gén. en <a href="#entry-varadamudraa" class="skt-link">varadamudrā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnatraya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnatraya</i> [<a href="#entry-traya" class="skt-link">traya</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnatraya" class="skt-link">n.</a> bd. les trois joyaux du bouddhisme: <a href="#entry-buddha" class="skt-link">buddha</a>, <a href="#entry-dharma" class="skt-link">dharma</a> et <a href="#entry-safgha" class="skt-link">saṅgha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. les trois joyaux du jaïnisme: <a href="#entry-samyagdarzana" class="skt-link">samyagdarśana</a>, <a href="#entry-samyagj_naana" class="skt-link">samyagjñāna</a> et <a href="#entry-samyakcaaritra" class="skt-link">samyakcāritra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ratnazaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ratnaśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-ratnazaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> gemmologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raucya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रौच्य</span> <i>raucya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-rucya" class="skt-link">rucya</a>] <a href="#entry-raucya" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Raucya «qui tient un bâton de <a href="#entry-bilva" class="skt-link">bilva</a>», 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raudra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रौद्र</span> <i>raudra</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">rudra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raudrā</i> <a href="#entry-raudrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raudrī</i> violent, furieux, sauvage, terrible; funeste — <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">n.</a> férocité, sauvagerie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [esthétique] le furieux, un des 9 modes artistiques [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>]; il évoque le sentiment d'agressivité; on lui associe la couleur rouge et la divinité <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> — <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Raudra «Furieux», personnifiant l'an 54 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>] — <a href="#entry-raudrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raudrī</i> myth. np. de Raudrī, <a href="#entry-zakti" class="skt-link">śakti</a> de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raudrakaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raudrakāla</i> [<a href="#entry-kaala_1" class="skt-link">kāla_1</a>] <a href="#entry-raudrakaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> soc. «temps de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>», moment du matin (variable) considéré comme néfaste pour commencer une entreprise.<br> <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">raudra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rauk_sya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रौक्ष्य</span> <i>raukṣya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ruuk_sa" class="skt-link">rūkṣa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-rauk_sya" class="skt-link">n.</a> sécheresse, rudesse; dureté, âpreté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raumaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रौमक</span> <i>raumaka</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-romaka" class="skt-link">romaka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raumaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raumaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raumikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raumikā</i> romain — <a href="#entry-raumaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> le latin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raupya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रौप्य</span> <i>raupya</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-ruupya" class="skt-link">rūpya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-raupya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-raupya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-raupyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raupyā</i> argenté; en argent — <a href="#entry-raupya" class="skt-link">n.</a> argent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-raupyapii_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raupyapīṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-pii_tha" class="skt-link">pīṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-raupyapii_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> géo. np. du Raupyapīṭha «Socle d'argent», centre de pélerinage du Sud Kanara à <a href="#entry-Uu_dupii" class="skt-link">Uḍupī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-raupya" class="skt-link">raupya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रव</span> <i>rava</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ru" class="skt-link">ru</a>] <a href="#entry-rava" class="skt-link">m.</a> cri, hurlement; bruit — <a href="#entry-ravas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ravas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ravas" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ravas</i> ifc. qui crie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rava_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रवण</span> <i>ravaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ru" class="skt-link">ru</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rava_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rava_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rava_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ravaṇā</i> qui hurle, qui crie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sonore, bruyant — <a href="#entry-rava_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> bruit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> coucou; divers animaux — <a href="#entry-rava_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> bronze.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रवि</span> <i>ravi</i> <a href="#entry-ravi" class="skt-link">m.</a> Soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Ravi, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>, le Soleil <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. symb. le nombre 12 (pour les 12 radieux [<a href="#entry-aadityaas" class="skt-link">ādityās</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravicandra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravicandra</i> [<a href="#entry-candra" class="skt-link">candra</a>] <a href="#entry-ravicandra" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du poète Ravicandra, qui donna un commentaire en <a href="#entry-zle_sa" class="skt-link">śleṣa</a> de l'<a href="#entry-Uamaruzataka" class="skt-link">Amaruśataka</a>, une version étant érotique et l'autre ascétique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravidina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravidina</i> [<a href="#entry-dina" class="skt-link">dina</a>] <a href="#entry-ravidina" class="skt-link">n.</a> Dimanche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravigupta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravigupta</i> [<a href="#entry-gupta" class="skt-link">gupta</a>] <a href="#entry-ravigupta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du lettré bd. Ravigupta (7⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle), auteur du <a href="#entry-Usiddhasaara" class="skt-link">Siddhasāra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravinandana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravinandana</i> [<a href="#entry-nandana" class="skt-link">nandana</a>] <a href="#entry-ravinandana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Ravinandana, épith. de <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a> «Fils du Soleil».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravisafkraanti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravisaṃkrānti</i> [<a href="#entry-safkraanti" class="skt-link">saṃkrānti</a>] <a href="#entry-ravisa_mkraanti" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. passage du soleil dans un nouveau signe du zodiaque.<br> <a href="#entry-ravi" class="skt-link">ravi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravivaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravivāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vaara" class="skt-link">vāra</a>] <a href="#entry-ravivaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> Dimanche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ravivaasara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ravivāsara</i> [<a href="#entry-vaasara" class="skt-link">vāsara</a>] <a href="#entry-ravivaasara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ravivaasara" class="skt-link">n.</a> Dimanche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rayi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रयि</span> <i>rayi</i> [<a href="#entry-rai" class="skt-link">rai</a>] <a href="#entry-rayi" class="skt-link">m.</a> biens, richesse <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Rayi, personnifiant la Fortune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-razanaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रशना</span> <i>raśanā</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-razmi" class="skt-link">raśmi</a>] <a href="#entry-razanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> corde, lanière <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rêne, longe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> corset; ceinture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rayon (not. de lumière).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-razmi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रश्मि</span> <i>raśmi</i> <a href="#entry-razmi" class="skt-link">m.</a> corde, ficelle; rêne, bride; laisse; baguette, fouet <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rayon de lumière; splendeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-razmimat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raśmimat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-razmimat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-razmimat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-razmimatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raśmimatī</i> qui irradie, rayonnant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> le Soleil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-razmivat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>raśmivat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-razmivat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-razmivat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-razmivatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>raśmivatī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-razmimat" class="skt-link">raśmimat</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-razmi" class="skt-link">raśmi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ra_nj">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रञ्ज्</span> <i>rañj</i> var. <i>raj</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(rajyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(rajyate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(rajati)</i> pr. md. <i>(rajate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rakta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> être coloré, se colorer; rougir, flamber <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être ému par ⟨i.⟩, s'émouvoir; s'éprendre de, trouver son plaisir à, aimer ⟨loc.⟩ — ca. <i>(rañjayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rañjita</i><i>)</i> teindre, colorer; colorer en rouge; illuminer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se concilier, satisfaire, gagner qqn. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ρηγος.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ra_njaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रञ्जक</span> <i>rañjaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ra_njaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rañjakī</i> qui colorie, qui teint <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui suscite l'amour; plaisant — <a href="#entry-ra_njaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rañjakī</i> teinturier; peintre — <a href="#entry-ra_njaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> cinabre; vermillon.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ra_njana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रञ्जन</span> <i>rañjana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ra_njana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rañjanī</i> qui colorie, qui teint — <a href="#entry-ra_njana" class="skt-link">n.</a> coloration; teinture; couleur; peinture <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> conciliation; fait de plaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phon. nasalisation — <a href="#entry-ra_njanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rañjanī</i> indigo; curcuma; safran — ifc. qui plaît à, qui charme, qui enchante.<br> <i>bhaktarañjanī</i> qui plaît aux fidèles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ra_njita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रञ्जित</span> <i>rañjita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ra_nj" class="skt-link">rañj</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ra_njita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ra_njitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rañjitā</i> coloré; teint, peint, teinté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> enchanté, charmé, content.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-re">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रे</span> <i>re</i> interj. (méprisant) peuh!<br> <i>re</i> <i>re</i> interj. marque le mépris et l'irrespect
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-re_nu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेणु</span> <i>reṇu</i> [<a href="#entry-ri" class="skt-link">ri</a>] <a href="#entry-re_nu" class="skt-link">m.</a> grain ou particule de sable, poussière, pollen; poudre — <a href="#entry-re_nu" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. np. de Reṇu «Poussière», épouse de <a href="#entry-Uvizvaamitra" class="skt-link">Viśvāmitra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Reṇu_1, épouse de <a href="#entry-Uprasenajit" class="skt-link">Prasenajit</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Ure_nukaa" class="skt-link">Reṇukā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-re_nuka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेणुक</span> <i>reṇuka</i> <a href="#entry-re_nuka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. formule magique (servant à bénir des armes) — <a href="#entry-re_nukaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>reṇukā</i> myth. np. de Reṇukā, fille du roi <a href="#entry-Uprasenajit" class="skt-link">Prasenajit</a> et de <a href="#entry-Ure_nu_1" class="skt-link">Reṇu_1</a>; elle épousa le sage [<a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a>] <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a>; coupable de concupiscence à regarder les ébats érotiques du roi <a href="#entry-Ukaartaviirya" class="skt-link">Kārtavīrya</a> prenant son bain (var. tombant amoureuse du <a href="#entry-gandharva" class="skt-link">gandharva</a> <a href="#entry-Ucitrasena" class="skt-link">Citrasena</a> en voyant son reflet dans la rivière), elle fut maudite par <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a> et condamnée à errer comme lépreuse; recouvrant sa beauté par sa dévotion à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, elle revint à <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a> qui ordonna à ses fils de la décapiter; seul le plus jeune, <a href="#entry-Uparazuraama" class="skt-link">Paraśurāma</a>, obéit, et obtint comme vœu de la ressusciter, d'être lavé du péché de matricide, et de rendre leurs sens à ses frères rendus stupides par malédiction de leur père pour lui avoir désobéi; on dit qu'au moment de son exécution, elle embrassa une intouchable nommée Ellammā, pour la remercier de sa sympathie, et elles furent décapitées ensemble; lorsque <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a> pardonna, ils remit les têtes sur les corps par ses pouvoirs magiques, mais se trompa; <a href="#entry-Ure_nukaa" class="skt-link">Reṇukā</a> revint à la vie avec un corps d'intouchable en tant que Māryammā, «Mère pluie», qui apporte la maladie (not. la variole) et la mort, mais aussi la guérison et la résurrection; un sanctuaire lui est consacré à Māhūr, dans le <a href="#entry-Umahaaraa_s_tra" class="skt-link">Mahārāṣṭra</a>; elle est l'objet d'un culte-pélerinage à Saundatti, au nord du <a href="#entry-Ukar_naa_taka" class="skt-link">Karṇāṭaka</a>, en commun avec Ellammā; on offre en sacrifice des boucs et des coqs à Māryammā, et des buffles à Ellammā; cf. <a href="#entry-Ukofka_naa" class="skt-link">Koṅkaṇā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rebha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेभ</span> <i>rebha</i> <a href="#entry-rebha" class="skt-link">m.</a> panégyriste, auteur de louanges <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du <a href="#entry-_r_si" class="skt-link">ṛṣi</a> Rebha, jeté dans un puits par les <a href="#entry-asuraas" class="skt-link">asurās</a>; il y resta 10 nuits et 9 jours jusqu'à être sauvé par les <a href="#entry-Uazvinau" class="skt-link">Aśvinau</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-reca">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेच</span> <i>reca</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ric" class="skt-link">ric</a>] <a href="#entry-reca" class="skt-link">m.</a> expiration.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-recaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेचक</span> <i>recaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ric" class="skt-link">ric</a>] a. <a href="#entry-recaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-recaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-recikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>recikā</i> qui vide, qui purge — <a href="#entry-recaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> expiration <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] lente expiration complète par le nez; opp. <a href="#entry-puuraka" class="skt-link">pūraka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> seringue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-recana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेचन</span> <i>recana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ric" class="skt-link">ric</a>] a. <a href="#entry-recana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-recana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-recanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>recanā</i> qui purge — <a href="#entry-recana" class="skt-link">n.</a> fait de vider, de purger, d'exhaler <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méd. purge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-recita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेचित</span> <i>recita</i> [ca. pp. <a href="#entry-ric" class="skt-link">ric</a>] a. <a href="#entry-recita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-recita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-recitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>recitā</i> vidé, purgé, nettoyé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> abandonné — <a href="#entry-recitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>recitā</i> nom d'une position des mains en dansant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rekhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेखा</span> <i>rekhā</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-lekhaa" class="skt-link">lekhā</a>] <a href="#entry-rekhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> ligne, rangée, série <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dessin, peinture; not. idole peinte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. droite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-repha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेफ</span> <i>repha</i> <a href="#entry-repha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. roulement du son ‘r’ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. la lettre ‘<i>ra</i>’.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-reta_hseka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>retaḥseka</i> var. <i>retasseka</i> [<a href="#entry-seka" class="skt-link">seka</a>] <a href="#entry-reta_hseka" class="skt-link">m.</a> éjaculation; rapport charnel.<br> <a href="#entry-retas" class="skt-link">retas</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-retas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेतस्</span> <i>retas</i> [<a href="#entry-ri" class="skt-link">ri</a><a href="#entry--tas" class="skt-link">-tas</a>] <a href="#entry-retas" class="skt-link">n.</a> flot, courant, libation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>retana</i> sperme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mercure (sperme d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> ou de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>).<br> <i>retaḥ</i> <i>sic</i> éjaculer.<br> <i>reto</i> <i>dhā</i> (r.) concevoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-revanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेवन्त</span> <i>revanta</i> [<a href="#entry-revat" class="skt-link">revat</a>] <a href="#entry-revanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Revanta «Splendide», fils de <a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a> et <a href="#entry-Usa_nj_naa" class="skt-link">Sañjñā</a>; il est le chef des esprits cachés [<a href="#entry-guhyakaas" class="skt-link">guhyakās</a>]; sa beauté stupéfiante égara <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Uekaviira" class="skt-link">Ekavīra</a>; dans le sud de l'Inde il est identifié avec Ayyanār-<a href="#entry-Uzaastaa" class="skt-link">Śāstā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-revat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेवत्</span> <i>revat</i> [<a href="#entry-rai" class="skt-link">rai</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-revat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-revat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-revatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>revatī</i> riche, prospère; abondant; splendide — <a href="#entry-revat" class="skt-link">n.</a> abondance, richesse, prospérité — <a href="#entry-revatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>revatī</i> astr. 26⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile ζ des Poissons; <a href="#entry-Upuu_saa" class="skt-link">Pūṣā</a> y préside; son symbole est une timbale <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> femme née sous le signe de cet astérisme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Revatī «Abondance», fille de <a href="#entry-Urevata" class="skt-link">Revata</a>; [BhP.] née à l'ère du <a href="#entry-tretaayuga" class="skt-link">tretāyuga</a>, elle alla avec son père à la cour de <a href="#entry-Ubrahmaa" class="skt-link">Brahmā</a> et quand ils revinrent sur Terre c'était le <a href="#entry-dvaaparayuga" class="skt-link">dvāparayuga</a>; les hommes étaient devenus plus petits, et pour marier cette géante son père ne trouva que <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> à sa taille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-revata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रेवत</span> <i>revata</i> [<a href="#entry-revat" class="skt-link">revat</a>] <a href="#entry-revata" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Revata «Prospère», fils d'<a href="#entry-Uaanarta" class="skt-link">Ānarta</a>; il est père de <a href="#entry-Urevatii" class="skt-link">Revatī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ri">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रि</span> <i>ri</i> var. <i>rī</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(riṇāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(riṇīte)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(rīyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rīṇa</i><i>)</i> relâcher, libérer, émettre, faire aller; détacher de ⟨abl.⟩; accorder <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se dissoudre, fondre, couler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ri_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिष्</span> <i>riṣ</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(riṣyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(riṣyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>riṣṭa</i><i>)</i> souffrir un dommage (matériel ou moral) — ca. <i>(reṣayati)</i> endommager; léser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ri_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिष्ट</span> <i>riṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ri_s" class="skt-link">riṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ri_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ri_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ri_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>riṣṭā</i> endommagé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ric">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिच्</span> <i>ric</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-ri" class="skt-link">ri</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. md. <i>(ricyate)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(aricat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(arīricat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rikta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ati</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> se vider, être vidé; être libéré ou exempt de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être détruit, périr — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(riṇakti)</i> laisser, lâcher, vider; évacuer, relâcher — ca. <i>(recayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>recita</i><i>)</i> vider; libérer, émettre; abandonner <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. λειπω; lat. <i>linquo</i>; all. leihen; ang. loan; ang. lend.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">री</span> <i>rī</i> cf. <a href="#entry-ri" class="skt-link">ri</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-riiti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रीति</span> <i>rīti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ri" class="skt-link">ri</a>] <a href="#entry-riiti" class="skt-link">f.</a> véd. mouvement; cours, courant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> méthode, moyen; usage, coutume; manière, style <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. style littéraire; mode de composition poétique <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> diction, manière de prononcer; il y en a trois principales: <a href="#entry-vaidarbhii" class="skt-link">vaidarbhī</a>, <a href="#entry-gau_dii" class="skt-link">gauḍī</a> et <a href="#entry-paa_ncaalii" class="skt-link">pāñcālī</a>, auxquelles on ajoute <a href="#entry-laa_tikaa" class="skt-link">lāṭikā</a>, <a href="#entry-aa_avantikaa" class="skt-link">āavantikā</a> et <a href="#entry-maagadhii" class="skt-link">māgadhī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bronze; laiton; rouille, métal oxydé.<br> <i>rītir</i> <i>ātmā</i> <i>kāvyasya</i> [<a href="#entry-Uvaamana_2" class="skt-link">Vāmana_2</a>] Le style est l'âme de la poésie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rikta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिक्त</span> <i>rikta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ric" class="skt-link">ric</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rikta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rikta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-riktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>riktā</i> vide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (feuille) vierge (d'inscription); nu (bras) — ifc. sans, dépourvu de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-riktha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिक्थ</span> <i>riktha</i> <a href="#entry-riktha" class="skt-link">n.</a> héritage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> capital; possessions; richesses, fortune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rikthagraaha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rikthagrāha</i> [<a href="#entry-graaha" class="skt-link">grāha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rikthagraaha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rikthagraaha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rikthagraahaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rikthagrāhā</i> qui reçoit un héritage, qui hérite de biens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rikthahara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rikthahara</i> [<a href="#entry-hara" class="skt-link">hara</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rikthahara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rikthahara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rikthaharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rikthaharā</i> qui reçoit la fortune en héritage.<br> <a href="#entry-riktha" class="skt-link">riktha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rip">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिप्</span> <i>rip</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-lip" class="skt-link">lip</a>] pp. <i>(</i><i>ripta</i><i>)</i> tromper.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ripu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिपु</span> <i>ripu</i> [<a href="#entry-rip" class="skt-link">rip</a><a href="#entry--u" class="skt-link">-u</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ripu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ripu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ripu" class="skt-link">f.</a> trompeur; faux — <a href="#entry-ripu" class="skt-link">m.</a> traître; adversaire, ennemi <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. planète hostile <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Ripu, 4⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ fils de <a href="#entry-Uyadu" class="skt-link">Yadu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ripughaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>ripughāta</i> [<a href="#entry-ghaata" class="skt-link">ghāta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ripughaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ripughaata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ripughaatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ripughātī</i> qui tue les ennemis.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-riputaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>riputā</i> [<a href="#entry--taa" class="skt-link">-tā</a>] <a href="#entry-riputaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> inimitié.<br> <a href="#entry-ripu" class="skt-link">ripu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rira_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिरंस्</span> <i>riraṃs</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-ram" class="skt-link">ram</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(riraṃsate)</i> désirer sexuellement.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rira_msaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिरंसा</span> <i>riraṃsā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rira_ms" class="skt-link">riraṃs</a>] <a href="#entry-rira_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir sexuel, lubricité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rira_msu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिरंसु</span> <i>riraṃsu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rira_ms" class="skt-link">riraṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rira_msu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rira_msu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rira_msu" class="skt-link">f.</a> [«qui veut jouir sexuellement»] lubrique, lascif.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-riraadhayi_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिराधयिष्</span> <i>rirādhayiṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-raadh" class="skt-link">rādh</a>] v. pr. <i>(rirādhayiṣati)</i> désirer accomplir; souhaiter réussir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-riraadhayi_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिराधयिषु</span> <i>rirādhayiṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-riraadhayi_s" class="skt-link">rirādhayiṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-riraadhayi_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-riraadhayi_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-riraadhayi_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> désireux de réussir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ro_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोष्</span> <i>roṣ_1</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(roṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>roṣita</i><i>)</i> exaspérer, irriter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ro_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोष्</span> <i>roṣ_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ro_s_1" class="skt-link">roṣ_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ro_s_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ro_s_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ro_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui met en colère, qui enrage.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ro_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोष</span> <i>roṣa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ro_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> colère; rage, passion, furie.<br> <i>roṣeṇa</i> adv. avec colère.<br> <i>roṣaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> être en colère (contre ⟨g. loc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ro_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोषण</span> <i>roṣaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ro_sa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ro_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ro_sa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>roṣaṇā</i> qui s'irrite, colérique; passionné (de ou contre ⟨g. iic.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ro_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोषित</span> <i>roṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ro_s_1" class="skt-link">roṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ro_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ro_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ro_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>roṣitā</i> irrité, exaspéré, excédé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ro_tii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोटी</span> <i>roṭī</i> <a href="#entry-ro_tii" class="skt-link">f.</a> pain <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>roṭikā</i> id. <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>roṭī</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rocana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोचन</span> <i>rocana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rocana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rocana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rocanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rocanā</i> brillant, lumineux; beau, charmant; tonique — <a href="#entry-rocana" class="skt-link">n.</a> éveil du désir pour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rocis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोचिस्</span> <i>rocis</i> [<a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rocis" class="skt-link">n.</a> éclat, splendeur; grâce.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rod">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोद्</span> <i>rod</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-rud_1" class="skt-link">rud_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(rodayati)</i> faire pleurer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rodana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोदन</span> <i>rodana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rud_1" class="skt-link">rud_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rodana" class="skt-link">n.</a> lamentation, pleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rodha_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोध</span> <i>rodha_1</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rudh_1" class="skt-link">rudh_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-rodha_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rodha_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rodhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rodhā</i> qui pousse, qui croît; qui monte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rodha_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोध</span> <i>rodha_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rudh_2" class="skt-link">rudh_2</a>] <a href="#entry-rodha_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de retenir ou d'enfermer; obstruction, blocus, siège; entrave; digue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rodhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोध्र</span> <i>rodhra</i> var. <i>lodhra</i> [<a href="#entry-rudhira" class="skt-link">rudhira</a>] <a href="#entry-rodhra" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Symplocos racemosa</i>, arbre à fleurs jaunes; son écorce sert à faire la poudre rouge lancée à <a href="#entry-holii" class="skt-link">holī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोग</span> <i>roga</i> [instr. <a href="#entry-ruj_1" class="skt-link">ruj_1</a>] <a href="#entry-roga" class="skt-link">m.</a> maladie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rogalak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rogalakṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-rogalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> méd. symptôme; pathologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rogamukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rogamukta</i> [<a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rogamukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rogamukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rogamuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rogamuktā</i> [«libéré de maladie»] convalescent, guéri.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rogavinizcaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rogaviniścaya</i> [<a href="#entry-vinizcaya" class="skt-link">viniścaya</a>] <a href="#entry-rogavinizcaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. np. du Rogaviniścaya de <a href="#entry-Umaadhava_2" class="skt-link">Mādhava_2</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Umaadhavanidaana" class="skt-link">Mādhavanidāna</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rogazama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rogaśama</i> [<a href="#entry-zama" class="skt-link">śama</a>] <a href="#entry-rogazama" class="skt-link">m.</a> méd. convalescence.<br> <a href="#entry-roga" class="skt-link">roga</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rogin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोगिन्</span> <i>rogin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruj_1" class="skt-link">ruj_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rogin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rogin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rogi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rogiṇī</i> malade.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोह</span> <i>roha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-roha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-roha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rohaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rohā</i> qui s'élève, qui grimpe — ifc. qui a pour monture — <a href="#entry-roha" class="skt-link">m.</a> ascension; élévation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> augmentation, croissance; germination <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bourgeon; pousse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roha_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोहण</span> <i>rohaṇa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-roha_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> moyen d'ascension <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> guérison (d'une blessure) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sperme — ifc. fait de monter sur ou de chevaucher — <a href="#entry-roha_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. np. de Rohaṇa, maître de <a href="#entry-Unaagasena" class="skt-link">Nāgasena</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>rohaṇaparvata</i>] géo. np. du Mont Rohaṇa_1, mod. Pic d'Adam à <a href="#entry-Ulafkaa" class="skt-link">Laṅkā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोहक</span> <i>rohaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rohaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rohaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rohakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rohakā</i> qui monte, qui s'élève — ifc. qui est monté sur, qui chevauche — <a href="#entry-rohaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. esprit, spectre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rohasena</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-rohasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. [<a href="#entry-Um_rcchaka_tikaa" class="skt-link">Mṛcchakaṭikā</a>] np. du jeune garçon Rohasena «Armée en herbe», fils de <a href="#entry-Ucaarudatta" class="skt-link">Cārudatta</a> et <a href="#entry-Udhuutaa" class="skt-link">Dhūtā</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-roha" class="skt-link">roha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohi_nii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोहिणी</span> <i>rohiṇī</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-rohita" class="skt-link">rohita</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohi_niiku_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rohiṇīkuṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-ku_n_da" class="skt-link">kuṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-rohi_niiku_n_da" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. np. de la fontaine de libération Rohiṇīkuṇḍa, située près de la colline de Nīlagiri, au <a href="#entry-Upuru_sottamak_setra" class="skt-link">Puruṣottamakṣetra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-rohi_nii" class="skt-link">rohiṇī</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohiitaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोहीतक</span> <i>rohītaka</i> [<a href="#entry-rohitaka" class="skt-link">rohitaka</a>] <a href="#entry-rohiitaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-rohitaka" class="skt-link">rohitaka</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
