    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोहित</span> <i>rohita</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-lohita" class="skt-link">lohita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rohita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rohita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rohi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rohiṇī</i> rouge — <a href="#entry-rohita" class="skt-link">m.</a> la couleur rouge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sang <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> natu. zoo. <i>Cyprinus rohita</i>, poisson rouge <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> véd. nom de Rohita «Le Rouge», forme du Soleil comme créateur du monde <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'arc d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> «Rouge» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. du prince <a href="#entry-Urohitaazva" class="skt-link">Rohitāśva</a> — <a href="#entry-rohi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rohiṇī</i> myth. np. de Rohiṇī «Rougeoyante», épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uu_saa" class="skt-link">Uṣā</a> l'Aurore, fille de <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a>, avec laquelle il commit l'inceste primordial lors de la Création; cf. <a href="#entry-m_rgaziras" class="skt-link">mṛgaśiras</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. Rohiṇī_1 «(Aldébaran l'étoile) Rouge», fille de <a href="#entry-Udak_sa" class="skt-link">Dakṣa</a>, épouse favorite de <a href="#entry-Usoma" class="skt-link">Soma</a>-<a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>; astr. sa symbolisation cosmologique en tant que 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à l'étoile Aldébaran; <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> y préside; son symbole est la brouette <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. Rohiṇī_2 «la (Vache) Rouge», fille de <a href="#entry-Ukazyapa" class="skt-link">Kaśyapa</a> et <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a>, mère de <a href="#entry-Ukaamadukh" class="skt-link">Kāmadukh</a> et du bétail <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] Rohiṇī_3, 2⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ épouse de <a href="#entry-Uvasudeva" class="skt-link">Vasudeva</a>, qui éleva <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> en secret; [HV.] elle incarne sur Terre <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ερυθρος; lat. <i>ruber</i>; ang. red; all. roth; fr. rouge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohitaazva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rohitāśva</i> [<a href="#entry-azva" class="skt-link">aśva</a>] <a href="#entry-rohitaazva" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du roi Rohitāśva «au Cheval rouge», fils de <a href="#entry-Uharizcandra" class="skt-link">Hariścandra</a> de la lignée solaire [<a href="#entry-suuryava_mza" class="skt-link">sūryavaṃśa</a>]; celui-ci acheta <a href="#entry-Uzuna_hzepa" class="skt-link">Śunaḥśepa</a> à son père pour le sacrifier à <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a> à sa place; <a href="#entry-Ucu_ncu" class="skt-link">Cuñcu</a> est son fils.<br> <a href="#entry-rohita" class="skt-link">rohita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rohitaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rohitaka</i> var. <i>rohītaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-rohitaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Aphanamixis polystachya</i> (ou <i>Andersonia rohitaka</i>), arbre dont l'écorce et les graines sont utilisés en médecine not. pour les affections de la rate <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. np. de la ville de Rohitaka, mod. Rohtak en <a href="#entry-Uhariyaa_na" class="skt-link">Hariyāṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rolamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोलम्ब</span> <i>rolamba</i> <a href="#entry-rolamba" class="skt-link">m.</a> abeille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rolambakadamba">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rolambakadamba</i> [<a href="#entry-kadamba" class="skt-link">kadamba</a>] <a href="#entry-rolambakadamba" class="skt-link">n.</a> essaim d'abeilles.<br> <a href="#entry-rolamba" class="skt-link">rolamba</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roma_nvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romaṇvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-roma_nvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-roma_nvat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-roma_nvatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>romaṇvatī</i> poilu.<br> <i>śepo</i> <i>romaṇvantau</i> <i>bhedau</i> <i>icchati</i> [RV.] Le pénis désire le vagin poilu.<br> <a href="#entry-roman" class="skt-link">roman</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roma_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोम</span> <i>roma_1</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-roman" class="skt-link">roman</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roma_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोम</span> <i>roma_2</i> <a href="#entry-roma_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> géo. la ville de Rome, l'état romain.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romaavalii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romāvalī</i> [<a href="#entry-aavalii" class="skt-link">āvalī</a>] <a href="#entry-romaavalii" class="skt-link">f.</a> ligne de poils verticale au-dessus du nombril; lit. elle est évoquée dans la poésie érotique.<br> <a href="#entry-roma_1" class="skt-link">roma_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romahar_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romaharṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-har_sa" class="skt-link">harṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-romahar_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> horripilation (de plaisir, de peur ou de froid); excitation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romahar_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romaharṣita</i> [<a href="#entry-har_sita" class="skt-link">harṣita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-romahar_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-romahar_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-romahar_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>romaharṣitā</i> qui a les poils qui se dressent (de plaisir, de peur); excité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-romaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>romakās</i> les Romains.<br> <a href="#entry-roma_2" class="skt-link">roma_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romakuupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romakūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-kuupa" class="skt-link">kūpa</a>] <a href="#entry-romakuupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-romakuupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> pore de la peau; syn. <a href="#entry-romarandhra" class="skt-link">romarandhra</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-roman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोमन्</span> <i>roman</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-loman" class="skt-link">loman</a>] <a href="#entry-roman" class="skt-link">n.</a> poil; antenne; toison, laine; fourrure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> plumes (d'oiseau); écailles (de poisson).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romantha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोमन्थ</span> <i>romantha</i> <a href="#entry-romantha" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait de ruminer, de mâcher — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(romanthāyate)</i> ruminer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>ruminare</i>; fr. ruminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romanthana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romanthana</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-romanthana" class="skt-link">n.</a> rumination.<br> <a href="#entry-romantha" class="skt-link">romantha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romapaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romapāda</i> var. <i>lomapāda</i> [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] <a href="#entry-romapaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Romapāda «au pied velu», roi des <a href="#entry-Uafgaas" class="skt-link">Aṅgās</a>, ami de <a href="#entry-Udazaratha" class="skt-link">Daśaratha</a>; sa capitale était <a href="#entry-Ucampaa" class="skt-link">Campā</a>; il donna sa fille <a href="#entry-Uzaantaa" class="skt-link">Śāntā</a> en mariage à l'ascète <a href="#entry-U_rzyaz_rfga" class="skt-link">Ṛśyaśṛṅga</a> pour faire cesser une sécheresse provoquée par sa faute.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romaraaji">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romarāji</i> [<a href="#entry-raaji" class="skt-link">rāji</a>] <a href="#entry-romaraaji" class="skt-link">f.</a> fine ligne de poils sur le ventre des femmes, sous le nombril <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>romarājī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romarandhra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romarandhra</i> [<a href="#entry-randhra" class="skt-link">randhra</a>] <a href="#entry-romarandhra" class="skt-link">n.</a> pore de la peau; syn. <a href="#entry-romakuupa" class="skt-link">romakūpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romavallii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romavallī</i> [<a href="#entry-vallii" class="skt-link">vallī</a>] <a href="#entry-romavallii" class="skt-link">f.</a> natu. bot. <i>Mucuna pruriens</i>, pois mascate; syn. <a href="#entry-kapikacchu" class="skt-link">kapikacchu</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-aatmaguptaa" class="skt-link">ātmaguptā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-romaza">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>romaśa</i> [<a href="#entry--za" class="skt-link">-śa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-romaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-romaza" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-romazaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>romaśā</i> poilu, velu; ébouriffé — <a href="#entry-romaza" class="skt-link">m.</a> mouton; cochon, sanglier; diverses plantes poilues.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rop">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोप्</span> <i>rop</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(ropayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ropita</i><i>)</i> élever, faire pousser; planter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ropa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोप</span> <i>ropa</i> [act. <a href="#entry-rop" class="skt-link">rop</a>] <a href="#entry-ropa" class="skt-link">m.</a> fait d'élever; plantation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ropa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोपण</span> <i>ropaṇa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rop" class="skt-link">rop</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ropa_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ropa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ropa_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ropaṇā</i> qui fixe; qui plante — <a href="#entry-ropa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> plantation.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ropita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रोपित</span> <i>ropita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rop" class="skt-link">rop</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ropita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ropita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ropitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ropitā</i> élevé; planté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रु</span> <i>ru</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(rauti)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ruta</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>vi</i><i>)</i> faire du bruit (bourdonner, gazouiller, etc.), retentir; crier, hurler; gémir — int. md. <i>(rorūyate)</i> hurler; rugir; tonner, vociférer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. ωρυομαι; lat. <i>raucus</i>, <i>rūmor</i>; fr. rumeur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ru_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुष्ट</span> <i>ruṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ru_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ru_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ru_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruṣṭā</i> irrité, mécontent; en colère, furieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ru_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुष्</span> <i>ruṣ_1</i> var. <i>ruś</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">4</span>] pr. <i>(ruṣyati)</i> pr. md. <i>(ruṣyate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ruṣita</i>, <i>ruṣta</i><i>)</i> être irrité, s'irriter (contre ⟨g.⟩) — ca. <i>(roṣayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>roṣita</i><i>)</i> exaspérer, irriter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ru_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुष्</span> <i>ruṣ_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ru_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> colère, fureur — <a href="#entry-ru_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruṣā</i> ifc. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ru_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुष</span> <i>ruṣa</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ru_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ru_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ru_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruṣā</i> qui s'irrite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ru_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुषित</span> <i>ruṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ru_s_1" class="skt-link">ruṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ru_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ru_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ru_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruṣitā</i> irrité (contre ⟨g. loc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruc_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुच्</span> <i>ruc_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. md. <i>(rocate)</i> pft. <i>(ruruce)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(arucat)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">5</span>] <i>(arociṣṭa)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rucita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>rucya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>abhi</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>vi</i><i>)</i> luire, briller; être beau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> sembler beau ou bon, être désirable, plaire (à ⟨dat. g.⟩) — ca. <i>(rocayati)</i> faire briller, illuminer; aimer, approuver <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. λευκος; lat. <i>lūceo</i>; ang. light; all. Licht; fr. luciole.<br> <i>tasmā</i> <i>upakhyānaṃ</i> <i>na</i> <i>rocate</i> l'histoire ne lui plaît pas.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruc_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुच्</span> <i>ruc_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ruc_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclat, lumière; beauté — ifc. ressemblance à.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rucaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुचक</span> <i>rucaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-rucaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> parure d'or; not. collier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> type d'homme; cf. <a href="#entry-pa_ncamanu_syavibhaaga" class="skt-link">pañcamanuṣyavibhāga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruci">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुचि</span> <i>ruci</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ruci" class="skt-link">f.</a> éclat, lustre; beauté, splendeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> goût pour, plaisir trouvé à ⟨loc. <a href="#entry-prati" class="skt-link">prati</a>-inf. iic.⟩.<br> <i>rucyā</i> i. adv. à son gré.<br> <i>svarucyā</i> adv. à son gré, à son bon plaisir.<br> <i>ruciṃ</i> <i>dā</i> plaire.<br> <i>rucaye</i> <i>bhū</i> plaire.<br> <i>rucim</i> <i>āvah</i> éveiller le désir de ⟨dat.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rucikara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rucikara</i> [<a href="#entry-kara_1" class="skt-link">kara_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rucikara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rucikara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rucikaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rucikarā</i> qui fait plaisir.<br> <a href="#entry-ruci" class="skt-link">ruci</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rucira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुचिर</span> <i>rucira</i> [<a href="#entry-ruci" class="skt-link">ruci</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rucira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rucira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruciraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rucirā</i> brillant (propre ou figuré); radieux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charmant, qui plaît (à ⟨g.⟩).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rucita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुचित</span> <i>rucita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rucita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rucita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rucitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rucitā</i> éclairé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> brillant; agréable, joli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aiguisé (appétit).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rucya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुच्य</span> <i>rucya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rucya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rucya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rucyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rucyā</i> brillant, radiant; plaisant, beau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tonique — <a href="#entry-rucya" class="skt-link">m.</a> syn. <a href="#entry-bilva" class="skt-link">bilva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rud_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुद्</span> <i>rud_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] pr. <i>(roditi)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(rudati)</i> pr. md. <i>(rudate)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(rodati)</i> pr. md. <i>(rodate)</i> imp. <i>(rodi)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rudita</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(rudat)</i> pleurer; gémir, se lamenter; crier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pleurer qqn. ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(rodayati)</i> faire pleurer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>rūdo</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rud_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुद्</span> <i>rud_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-rud_1" class="skt-link">rud_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-rud_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rud_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rud_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui crie; qui pleure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुदत्</span> <i>rudat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-rud_1" class="skt-link">rud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rudat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rudat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rudatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rudatī</i> qui pleure.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुद्ध</span> <i>ruddha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rudh_2" class="skt-link">rudh_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruddhā</i> assiégé, cerné; arrêté, barré, bloqué, empêché, entravé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudh_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुध्</span> <i>rudh_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(rodhati)</i> croître, pousser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudh_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुध्</span> <i>rudh_2</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">7</span>] pr. <i>(ruṇaddhi)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(rundhati)</i> pr. md. <i>(runddhe)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(arodhat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>ruddha</i><i>)</i> ppr. <i>(rundhat)</i> inf. <i>(roddhum)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>ni</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> barrer, arrêter, bloquer, entraver; obstruer, empêcher, retenir, enfermer <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> assiéger, cerner; investir, opprimer — ps. <i>(rudhyate)</i> être combattu; combattre — ca. <i>(rodhayati)</i> faire arrêter; faire assiéger, faire attaquer; tourmenter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudhira">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुधिर</span> <i>rudhira</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-rohita" class="skt-link">rohita</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rudhira" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rudhira" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rudhiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rudhirā</i> rouge; rouge-sang; saignant — <a href="#entry-rudhira" class="skt-link">n.</a> sang <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> safran — <a href="#entry-rudhira" class="skt-link">m.</a> np. de Rudhira, la «Rouge» planète Mars; cf. <a href="#entry-Umafgala" class="skt-link">Maṅgala</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>ruber</i>; ang. red; fr. rouge.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudhiraak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudhirākṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-rudhiraak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> cornaline.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudhirodgaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudhirodgārin</i> [<a href="#entry-udgaarin" class="skt-link">udgārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rudhirodgaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rudhirodgaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rudhirodgaarinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rudhirodgārinī</i> qui émet du sang — <a href="#entry-rudhirodgaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. np. de Rudhirodgārī «qui perd du sang», personnifiant l'an 57 d'un cycle de vie [<a href="#entry-sa_mvatsara" class="skt-link">saṃvatsara</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-rudhira" class="skt-link">rudhira</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुदित</span> <i>rudita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-rud_1" class="skt-link">rud_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rudita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rudita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruditaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruditā</i> qui pleure <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> déploré — <a href="#entry-rudita" class="skt-link">n.</a> pleurs.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुद्र</span> <i>rudra</i> [<a href="#entry-rud_1" class="skt-link">rud_1</a><a href="#entry--ra" class="skt-link">-ra</a>] <a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. véd. np. de Rudra «Tourmenteur» [«qui fait pleurer»], ou «Furieux» [«dont la nature est <a href="#entry-raudra" class="skt-link">raudra</a>»]; sauvage chef des souffles [<a href="#entry-marut" class="skt-link">marut</a>], divinité terrible; aussi épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a> et prototype véd. de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; en tant que <a href="#entry-Um_rgavyaadha" class="skt-link">Mṛgavyādha</a> il perça <a href="#entry-Uprajaapati" class="skt-link">Prajāpati</a> de sa flèche lors de l'inceste primordial, et l'épingla au Ciel comme <a href="#entry-Um_rgaziras" class="skt-link">Mṛgaśiras</a>; astr. lui-même préside le <a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a> <a href="#entry-Uaardraa" class="skt-link">Ārdrā</a> (Bételgeuse) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. il incarne la Colère métaphysique; à la fin du Monde [<a href="#entry-kalpaanta" class="skt-link">kalpānta</a>] il émane des têtes du dragon d'éternité <a href="#entry-Uananta" class="skt-link">Ananta</a> pour détruire l'Univers dégradé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>rudrās</i> myth. les Rudrās, aspects de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>, fils de <a href="#entry-Usurabhii" class="skt-link">Surabhī</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-ekaadazarudraas" class="skt-link">ekādaśarudrās</a> — <a href="#entry-rudraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rudrā</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Rudrā, l'une des 7 «mères (dangereuses) aux enfants» [<a href="#entry-zizumaataras" class="skt-link">śiśumātaras</a>] associées à <a href="#entry-Uskanda" class="skt-link">Skanda</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudra_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुद्रट</span> <i>rudraṭa</i> <a href="#entry-rudra_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du poète Rudraṭa; il vivait au <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> au 9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; on lui doit le traité de poétique <a href="#entry-Ukaavyaalafkaara_1" class="skt-link">Kāvyālaṃkāra_1</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudraabhi_seka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrābhiṣeka</i> [<a href="#entry-abhi_seka" class="skt-link">abhiṣeka</a>] <a href="#entry-rudraabhi_seka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. rite d'aspersion continue du <a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> dans un sanctuaire shivaïte.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudraak_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrākṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ak_sa" class="skt-link">akṣa</a>] <a href="#entry-rudraak_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Elæocarpus sphæricus</i>, petit arbre aux feuilles lancéolées, aux fleurs blanches groupées; son fruit, une graine brune et ridée, est utilisé en médecine — <a href="#entry-rudraak_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> [«œil de <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a>»] sa graine, dont on fait des chapelets; myth. elle est sacrée à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, car issue des larmes que <a href="#entry-Urudra" class="skt-link">Rudra</a> versa quand il dut détruire <a href="#entry-Utripura" class="skt-link">Tripura</a>; syn. <a href="#entry-maalaaphala" class="skt-link">mālāphala</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudraak_samaalaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrākṣamālā</i> [<a href="#entry-maalaa" class="skt-link">mālā</a>] <a href="#entry-rudraak_samaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> chapelet de <a href="#entry-rudraak_sa" class="skt-link">rudrākṣa</a>, aussi porté en collier ou sur le chignon par les fidèles de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>; il est dit réchauffer le corps.<br> <a href="#entry-rudraak_sa" class="skt-link">rudrākṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudraambhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrāmbhā</i> [<a href="#entry-ambaa" class="skt-link">ambā</a>] <a href="#entry-rudraambhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Uraa_nii" class="skt-link">Rāṇī</a> Rudrāmbā ou Rudramadevī, fille du roi des <a href="#entry-Ukaakatiiyaas" class="skt-link">Kākatīyās</a> Gaṇapatideva qui régnait à <a href="#entry-Uekazilaa" class="skt-link">Ekaśilā</a> (mod. Warangal); celui-ci n'ayant pas d'héritier mâle, il organisa une cérémonie de <a href="#entry-putrikaa" class="skt-link">putrikā</a> pour la nommer prince héritier sous le nom de <a href="#entry-Urudradeva" class="skt-link">Rudradeva</a>; elle lui succéda à l'âge de 14 ans, et régna de 1259 à 1289; elle épousa le prince <a href="#entry-Ucaalukya" class="skt-link">Cālukya</a> de l'Est <a href="#entry-Uviirabhadra_1" class="skt-link">Vīrabhadra_1</a>; son règne futt une succession de guerres; elle mourut en combattant le <a href="#entry-kaayastha" class="skt-link">kāyastha</a> rebelle Ambadeva; son petit-fils <a href="#entry-Uprataaparudra_1" class="skt-link">Pratāparudra_1</a> lui succéda.<br> <a href="#entry-rudra" class="skt-link">rudra</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudradeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudradeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-rudradeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du roi Rudradeva des <a href="#entry-Ukaakatiiyaas" class="skt-link">Kākatīyās</a> (1259–1289), en fait la reine <a href="#entry-Urudraambaa" class="skt-link">Rudrāmbā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudraga_nikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudragaṇikā</i> [<a href="#entry-ga_nikaa" class="skt-link">gaṇikā</a>] <a href="#entry-rudraga_nikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. prostituée sacrée d'un temple <a href="#entry-zaiva" class="skt-link">śaiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudrah_rdayopani_sad">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrahṛdayopaniṣad</i> [<a href="#entry-h_rdaya" class="skt-link">hṛdaya</a>-<a href="#entry-upani_sad" class="skt-link">upaniṣad</a>] <a href="#entry-rudrah_rdayopani_sad" class="skt-link">f.</a> lit. np. de la Rudrahṛdayopaniṣad.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudramuhuurta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudramuhūrta</i> [<a href="#entry-muhuurta" class="skt-link">muhūrta</a>] <a href="#entry-rudramuhuurta" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. période d'un laps [<a href="#entry-muhuurta" class="skt-link">muhūrta</a>] (48') précédant le lever du soleil.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudrasaavar_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrasāvarṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-saavar_na" class="skt-link">sāvarṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-rudrasaavar_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Rudrasāvarṇa, 12⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ <a href="#entry-manu" class="skt-link">manu</a> du <a href="#entry-kalpa" class="skt-link">kalpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudravii_naa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudravīṇā</i> [<a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a>] <a href="#entry-rudravii_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> luth [<a href="#entry-vii_naa" class="skt-link">vīṇā</a>] d'Inde du Nord.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rudrayaamala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rudrayāmala</i> [<a href="#entry-yaamala" class="skt-link">yāmala</a>] <a href="#entry-rudrayaamala" class="skt-link">n.</a> [<i>rudrayāmalatantra</i>] lit. np. du Rudrayāmala <a href="#entry-tantra" class="skt-link">tantra</a>; ce texte antique contient not. des enseignements médicaux et chimiques, mais aussi des techniques de libération ésotériques <a href="#entry-kaula" class="skt-link">kaula</a>; un autre texte homonyme date du 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; cf. <a href="#entry-Uparaatri_mzikaa" class="skt-link">Parātriṃśikā</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvij_naanabhairava" class="skt-link">Vijñānabhairava</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rug_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुग्ण</span> <i>rugṇa</i> a. <a href="#entry-rug_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rug_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rug_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rugṇā</i> cassé, tordu; blessé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> malade; infirme — <a href="#entry-rug_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> fissure, crevasse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruh_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुह्</span> <i>ruh_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-rudh_1" class="skt-link">rudh_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(rohati)</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(ruhati)</i> pft. <i>(ruroha)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">2</span>] <i>(aruhat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rūḍha</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>adhi</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> croître, naître, apparaître; se lever (Soleil) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> monter, germer, pousser <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cicatriser, guérir (blessure) — ca. <i>(rohayati)</i> ca. <i>(ropayati)</i> élever, faire pousser; planter — dés. <i>(rurukṣati)</i> désirer croître.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruh_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुह्</span> <i>ruh_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ruh_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> montée, croissance; érection — ifc. qui fait monter, qui fait jaillir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुह</span> <i>ruha</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ruha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruhā</i> qui fait monter, qui fait jaillir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui croît dans.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruj_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुज्</span> <i>ruj_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. <i>(rujati)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ā</i><i>)</i> briser, détruire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> faire du mal, tourmenter ⟨acc. g.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. λυγρος; lat. <i>lūgeo</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruj_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुज्</span> <i>ruj_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruj_1" class="skt-link">ruj_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ruj_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> maladie — ifc. qui brise, qui détruit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rukma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुक्म</span> <i>rukma</i> [<a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a><a href="#entry--ma" class="skt-link">-ma</a>] <a href="#entry-rukma" class="skt-link">n.</a> or — <a href="#entry-rukma" class="skt-link">m.</a> ornement d'or.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rukmin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुक्मिन्</span> <i>rukmin</i> [<a href="#entry-ruc_1" class="skt-link">ruc_1</a><a href="#entry--min" class="skt-link">-min</a>] <a href="#entry-rukmin" class="skt-link">n.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Rukmī «Orné d'or», fils de <a href="#entry-Ubhii_smaka" class="skt-link">Bhīṣmaka</a>, adversaire de <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, vaincu par <a href="#entry-Ubalaraama" class="skt-link">Balarāma</a> — <a href="#entry-rukmi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rukmiṇī</i> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Rukmiṇī, sa sœur; promise par son père à <a href="#entry-Uzizupaala" class="skt-link">Śiśupāla</a>, elle aimait en secret <a href="#entry-Uk_r_s_na" class="skt-link">Kṛṣṇa</a>, qui l'enleva à sa demande; par lui elle enfanta de <a href="#entry-Upradyumna" class="skt-link">Pradyumna</a>; on l'identifie à <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुम</span> <i>ruma</i> <a href="#entry-ruma" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [RV.] np. de Ruma, compagnon d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. nom d'une montagne contenant des mines de sel — <a href="#entry-rumaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rumā</i> géo. np. de la rivière Rumā <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> géo. nom d'un lac salé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruma_nvat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rumaṇvat</i> [<a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] <a href="#entry-ruma_nvat" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Rumaṇvān, fils aîné de <a href="#entry-Ujamadagni" class="skt-link">Jamadagni</a>; [<a href="#entry-Uratnaavalii_1" class="skt-link">Ratnāvalī_1</a>] il devint ministre du roi <a href="#entry-Uudayana" class="skt-link">Udayana</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-ruma" class="skt-link">ruma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-rundhat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुन्धत्</span> <i>rundhat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-rudh_2" class="skt-link">rudh_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-rundhat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-rundhat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-rundhatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rundhatī</i> obstruant, bloquant, empêchant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruru">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुरु</span> <i>ruru</i> [red. <a href="#entry-ru" class="skt-link">ru</a>] <a href="#entry-ruru" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. zoo. <i>Cervus duvauceli</i>, cerf barasingha <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de l'ermite Ruru, fils de <a href="#entry-Upramati" class="skt-link">Pramati</a> et de <a href="#entry-Uprataapii" class="skt-link">Pratāpī</a>; à l'ermitage de Sthūlakeśa il tomba amoureux de la nymphe <a href="#entry-Upramadvaraa" class="skt-link">Pramadvarā</a>; le jour de leur mariage, celle-ci mourut d'une morsure de serpent; <a href="#entry-Ururu" class="skt-link">Ruru</a> la ressuscita en lui offrant la moitié de sa vie; <a href="#entry-Uzunaka" class="skt-link">Śunaka</a> est leur fils; <a href="#entry-Ururu" class="skt-link">Ruru</a> était devenu l'ennemi des serpents qu'il voulait exterminer; un jour il rencontra un serpent <a href="#entry-dundubha" class="skt-link">dundubha</a>, qui était <a href="#entry-Usahasrapaada" class="skt-link">Sahasrapāda</a> maudit par <a href="#entry-Ukhagama" class="skt-link">Khagama</a>, et qui reprit forme humaine pour l'en dissuader <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de l'ondin [<a href="#entry-daanava" class="skt-link">dānava</a>] Ruru_1 «Démon cerf»; il fut vaincu par la Déesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruu_dha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूढ</span> <i>rūḍha</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruu_dha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruu_dha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruu_dhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūḍhā</i> monté, gravi; poussé, développé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> répandu, notoire; traditionnel, conventionnel <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. idiomatique.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruu_dhaartha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūḍhārtha</i> [<a href="#entry-artha" class="skt-link">artha</a>] <a href="#entry-ruu_dhaartha" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. sens conventionnel d'un mot.<br> <a href="#entry-ruu_dha" class="skt-link">rūḍha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruu_dhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूढि</span> <i>rūḍhi</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ruh_1" class="skt-link">ruh_1</a>] <a href="#entry-ruu_dhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> montée, développement; progrès <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> notoriété; usage, convention, tradition <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>rūḍhilakṣaṇā</i>] gram. sens traditionnel d'un mot, sens figé (opp. <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a> sens étymologique).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruuk_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूक्ष</span> <i>rūkṣa</i> a. <a href="#entry-ruuk_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruuk_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruuk_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūkṣā</i> sec, aride; émacié <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> âpre; rugueux <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rude, brutal, cruel — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(rūkṣayati)</i> être rude; rudoyer; injurier, offenser.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruumaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूमा</span> <i>rūmā</i> <a href="#entry-ruumaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uraamaaya_na" class="skt-link">Rām.</a>] np. de Rūmā, épouse de <a href="#entry-Usugriiva" class="skt-link">Sugrīva</a> puis <a href="#entry-Uvaalii" class="skt-link">Vālī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruup">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूप्</span> <i>rūp</i> [vn. <i>rūpa</i>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(rūpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>rūpita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ni</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i><i>)</i> figurer, représenter (not. sur la scène), exprimer par gestes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूप</span> <i>rūpa</i> <a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> figure, forme; signe <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apparence, extérieur, image; couleur; espèce <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> grâce, beauté <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] de la vue [<a href="#entry-cak_sus" class="skt-link">cakṣus</a>]; son régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] est <a href="#entry-Uaaditya" class="skt-link">Āditya</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-vaize_sika" class="skt-link">vaiśeṣika</a>] la propriété [<a href="#entry-gu_na" class="skt-link">guṇa</a>] de la forme ou aspect <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. la forme, un des 5 agrégats [<a href="#entry-skandha" class="skt-link">skandha</a>] de l'ego — ifc. semblable à (comme le fr.-aire: <i>sūryarūpa</i> solaire).<br> <i>rūpaṃ</i> <i>kṛ</i> prendre la forme de [nom.].<br> <i>kṛṣṇo</i> <i>rūpaṃ</i> <i>kṛtvā</i> ayant pris une forme noire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपण</span> <i>rūpaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> illustration; métaphore <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> investigation, examen.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupaaruupa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpārūpa</i> [<a href="#entry-aruupa" class="skt-link">arūpa</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupaaruupa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupaaruupa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupaaruupaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpārūpā</i> phil. à la fois avec forme et sans forme; se dit de <a href="#entry-Ucandramauliizvara" class="skt-link">Candramaulīśvara</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupaavali">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpāvali</i> [<a href="#entry-aavali" class="skt-link">āvali</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupaavali" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. liste de formes fléchies du sanskrit <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>rūpāvalī</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupaavataara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpāvatāra</i> [<a href="#entry-avataara" class="skt-link">avatāra</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupaavataara" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Rūpāvatāra, grammaire du sanskrit ré-arrangée par sujets sous forme de catéchisme (10⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupabheda">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpabheda</i> [<a href="#entry-bheda" class="skt-link">bheda</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupabheda" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. la science des formes, un <a href="#entry-_sa_dafgazilpa" class="skt-link">ṣaḍaṅgaśilpa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupacandrikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpacandrikā</i> [<a href="#entry-candrikaa" class="skt-link">candrikā</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupacandrikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. np. de la Rūpacandrikā «Lumière sur la forme (du mot)», ouvrage de morphology de 1947 donnant les paradigmes de flexion du sanskrit.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupadhaarin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpadhārin</i> [<a href="#entry-dhaarin" class="skt-link">dhārin</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupadhaarin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupadhaarin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupadhaari_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpadhāriṇī</i> qui assume la forme de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupadhara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpadhara</i> [<a href="#entry-dhara" class="skt-link">dhara</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ruupadhara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupadhara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupadharaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpadharā</i> qui a la forme de; de la couleur de.<br> <i>gorūpadhara</i> a. m. n. en forme de vache.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupajiiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpajīva</i> [<a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupajiiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupajiiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupajiivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpajīvā</i> soc. [«qui vit de ses charmes»] qui vit de la prostitution — <a href="#entry-ruupajiivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpajīvā</i> prostituée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपक</span> <i>rūpaka</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a><a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpakā</i> allégorique, figuratif — <a href="#entry-ruupakaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpakā</i> ifc. qui a la forme de; similaire à — <a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> pièce (d'or ou d'argent), monnaie — <a href="#entry-ruupaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> lit. rhét. [<a href="#entry-alafkaara" class="skt-link">alaṃkāra</a>] identification métaphorique, où l'on compare l'<a href="#entry-upameya" class="skt-link">upameya</a> à l'<a href="#entry-upamaana" class="skt-link">upamāna</a>; simile, allégorie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> image; ressemblance; signe; symptôme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lit. l'un des 10 genres majeurs de théâtre, le plus estimé étant le <a href="#entry-naa_taka" class="skt-link">nāṭaka</a>; cf. <a href="#entry-Udazaruupaka" class="skt-link">Daśarūpaka</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> mus. cycle rythmique [<a href="#entry-taala" class="skt-link">tāla</a>] comprenant 1 <a href="#entry-druta" class="skt-link">druta</a> et 1 <a href="#entry-laghu" class="skt-link">laghu</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpakāra</i> [<a href="#entry-kaara" class="skt-link">kāra</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> sculpteur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupama_njarii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpamañjarī</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_njarii" class="skt-link">mañjarī</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupama_njarii" class="skt-link">f.</a> gram. np. de la Rūpamañjarī, ouvrage listant les formes morphologiques.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupasam_rddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpasamṛddha</i> [<a href="#entry-sam_rddha" class="skt-link">samṛddha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupasam_rddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupasam_rddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupasam_rddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpasamṛddhā</i> soc. de forme parfaite; excellent d'aspect.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupasam_rddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpasamṛddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-sam_rddhi" class="skt-link">samṛddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupasam_rddhi" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. perfection formelle (not. d'un acte rituel).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupasiddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpasiddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-siddhi" class="skt-link">siddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupasiddhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> gram. mod. traité donnant les paramètres de génération morphologique des racines.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupavat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपवत्</span> <i>rūpavat</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a><a href="#entry--vat" class="skt-link">-vat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupavat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupavat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupavatii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpavatī</i> qui a forme, incorporé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> beau, joli — ifc. ayant la forme ou les traits de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupavattara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपवत्तर</span> <i>rūpavattara</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-ruupavat" class="skt-link">rūpavat</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupavattara" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupavattara" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupavattaraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpavattarā</i> plus beau que.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपि</span> <i>rūpi</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-ruupin" class="skt-link">rūpin</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupidaaraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpidāraka</i> [<a href="#entry-daaraka" class="skt-link">dāraka</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupidaaraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> joli garçon.<br> <a href="#entry-ruupi" class="skt-link">rūpi</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपिन्</span> <i>rūpin</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpiṇī</i> corporel; qui a une telle forme, beau, joli — ifc. qui a la forme de, caractérisé par — <a href="#entry-ruupi_nii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpiṇī</i> [<a href="#entry-kaamazaastra" class="skt-link">kāmaśāstra</a>] l'un des 4 types de femmes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूपित</span> <i>rūpita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ruup" class="skt-link">rūp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpitā</i> représenté; exhibé; imaginé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूप्य</span> <i>rūpya</i> [<a href="#entry-ruupa" class="skt-link">rūpa</a><a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpyā</i> qui a une belle forme, joli <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> imprimé, gravé — <a href="#entry-ruupya" class="skt-link">n.</a> argent (métal) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pièce de monnaie frappée [«portant une effigie»] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. roupie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupyaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpyaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupyaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupyaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupyakii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpyakī</i> d'argent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupyamaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpyamaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ruupyamaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupyamaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ruupyamayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>rūpyamayī</i> en argent; contenant de l'argent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruupyaratnapariik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>rūpyaratnaparīkṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-ratnapariik_saa" class="skt-link">ratnaparīkṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-ruupyaratnapariik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> l'examen de monnaies et de joyaux, l'un des 64 arts [<a href="#entry-kalaa" class="skt-link">kalā</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-ruupya" class="skt-link">rūpya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ruzama">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुशम</span> <i>ruśama</i> <a href="#entry-ruzama" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [RV.] np. de Ruśama, compagnon d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> — <a href="#entry-ruzamaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>ruśamā</i> myth. np. de Ruśamā, qui fit un pari avec <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> sur qui ferait le plus vite le tour de la Terre; pendant qu'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> faisait le tour de la Terre, <a href="#entry-Uruzamaa" class="skt-link">Ruśamā</a> fit le tour du <a href="#entry-Ukuruk_setra" class="skt-link">Kurukṣetra</a> et gagna.
  </div>
</div>
